| 513 | 1,501–1,503 | **The Only Instrument She Has** | **the count she has stopped keeping toward anything and started keeping as what is**; six things, and the sixth points at one person; her brother named once in her own want and not out loud; and the decision to walk to the end of the long road |
| 514 | 1,504–1,507 | **A Boy Of Fifteen At The Gate** | **structural mouth four of five**, in five short sentences across four minutes; eleven miles of made road counted with a bar and four carts on it in two days; the chain lifted three times for nothing; and three hundred yards east of the turnstile, a wall set in the ground with a lit window behind it |
| 515 | 1,508–1,513 | **He Asks The Carrier** | **it takes him five days**, and the finding that the misreading was an index: eleven people watching a girl with a bar is a thing every one of them told somebody about; **he says nothing at all in this chapter**; and he puts his hand on the strap of the book and leaves it in the bag |
| 516 | 1,514 | **One, And Not A Word** | **the batch midpoint, shown and not said.** Two hundred and fifty-eight days, derived on the page. He writes forty seconds, turns a clean page face up on a bench and shuts the book. **The price is a number of one and the page has no second line on it.** She does not touch it — her hand will not take a gift — and leaves it on the bench in the dew and walks east |
| 517 | 1,515–1,520 | **East With A Number In Her Hand** | five days and eleven miles, the woman of fifty and the man of fifty with four boards, **the misreading not corrected and not correctable**, the number she cannot argue with, **the fear named once out loud in the open**, and a wall set in the ground with a light in a window behind it |
| 518 | 1,521 | **What She Told Him To Do** | **the batch climax and its irreversible step.** *Open it*, said once, on the boards, with the latch down, knowing the number. **The fifth frame scene**, away from her hearing, present tense throughout: a doorway with nothing under his feet, one figure on the far side of it, and the answer that a wall opened from the inside always leaves whoever opened it on the wrong side. **He opens it, makes no sound, and steps through. One person stays wrong-sided, permanently, and it is him** |
| 519 | 1,525–1,527 | **A Light Behind The Wall** | three days in a nine-foot room, **the first empty chair in this book that is not a death and is worse than one**; eleven words said once to a wall at a hundred yards; and at the fourth hour she sits in it and gets up and squares it back against the wall |
| 520 | 1,529 | **The First Thing He Says** | **she says it out loud at a gate in daylight in front of three people she did not ask for**, and **he speaks to her for the first time in over two hundred days — two hundred and seventy-three days, derived in the same sentence — and it is two words: *It was mine.*** It saves nobody. She is not grateful and does not thank him |
| 521 | 1,532–1,541 | **The Ground Finds Out** | the misreading ends **because the truth turned out to look like the mistake and not because anybody was corrected**; the carrier says good at ten feet; the store woman leaves a loaf on the counter and does not take it off again; **and being believed, for the wrong reason, is the strangest thing that has happened to her in a year** |
| 522 | 1,542–1,556 | **She Does Not Ask Him Back** | twelve mornings in an opening in a wall at the fourth hour; **there is a way to ask him and no way for the sentence to arrive**; she could un-say it and it would change nothing, and not trying is the whole of what is left to decide; and **the first person she has hurt on purpose who did not go somewhere to get away from it** |
| 523 | 1,557–1,561 | **Two Miles That Go Nowhere** | **Severance 3 at the fourth hour of the eighteenth, alone on flat ground at a turn, seventy-three days after Severance 2 and the shortest gap of the run**; the bracket; **she cannot go back down the road she came** — the lane walked four times in one day, in exactly the time it has always taken, going nowhere she has been; and she does not weep about it |

### What the batch established, for whoever drafts Batch 0004

- **Severance 3 is on the page** at day 1,561, seventy-three days after Severance 2, and **the volume's three stages now stand 149 days apart first to third and 270 from Nascent Soul 9.**
- **The wall is open.** He is on the far side of it, alive, and cannot come back across. **The room is nine feet by eleven and she sleeps on its boards; the chair is still in it and she has not sat in it since the thirteenth.**
- **She knows the number of one was him.** She said it out loud at the gate in daylight and nobody on nine days of road believes a word of it.
- **The misreading has ended, on the ground's own terms, and the ground believes her for the wrong reason.**
- **The entry itself is still not written.** Her ch 474 page is in the book; **the rule is written once and Batch 0004 owns it at ch 536.**
- **The Hunter is not in this batch.** His second and last Volume 9 page is Batch 0004's, at the mouth of ch 531, and she asks him there.

### Deviations from the plan, recorded rather than hidden

1. **The long road is eleven miles from the crossing to the gate, not four.** Ch 504, on the page and not reopened, gives
   the long road as eleven miles going east to a gate with a chain on it, and the batch follows the page and derives the
   figure on the page at ch 514 with the bar used as the measure. Full reasoning at `state/continuity.md` §*Three
   readings taken against the outline*.
2. **Ch 520 does not name the third cost.** The card carries *the road behind her is not hers any more* in its
   information line; Severance 3's cost is named once, in its own stage chapter, at ch 523, and ch 520 carries the age,
   the stages, the wages and the body and leaves the road alone.
3. **Ch 531's room.** The demand at its maximum in Batch 0004 will be made at the opened wall and not in the room, because
   the man on the wrong side cannot come back across. Recorded so Batch 0004 does not re-open the chapter and find a
   contradiction.

**Next:** `workspace/volume-09-close/PROMPT.md`, the volume close, which audits ch 479–538 and writes no chapter prose. *Volume 9 Batch 0004 is on the page; the pointer this line carried to `workspace/volume-09-batch-0004/PROMPT.md` has been executed in full.*

## Volume 9 Batch 0004 — *The Warden*, ch 524–538, day 1,562–1,598 — WRITTEN, VOLUME 9 CLOSED ON THE PAGE

**Pressure: surviving being kept.** Fifteen chapters, **28,734 words of body prose on method B
(every line not beginning `#`)**, average **~1,916**, range 1,643 at ch 534 to 2,149 at ch 533. Inside the volume's own
band: 27,000–30,000 per batch with a hard floor of 26,000 and a chapter floor of 1,300, and nothing in the batch is under
1,640 or over 2,150. **Zero blockquotes, code blocks, tables or horizontal rules; zero bracketed layer lines in fifteen
chapters; zero bold spans and two italic spans, both the word *lost*, at ch 535 and ch 536; zero straight quotes and zero
straight apostrophes; every speech closed; longest paragraph 89 words and none over 120; fifteen new titles, unique against
all 523 standing and against each other, longest 30 characters.**

### One line per chapter

| Ch | Days | Title | What changes on the page |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 524 | 1,562–1,564 | **Nothing Holds Her Here** | sixteen nights into a room somebody is letting her have and none of it is hers; she sleeps on the boards on purpose, goes out of the opening at the fourth hour and does not knock, and starts eleven days of working out how to be worth the price of one |
| 525 | 1,565–1,567 | **The Chair She Will Not Sit In** | she stops asking for an answer and names what she wants instead — to be asked for something, once; the blank book on the table she will not take; and the finding that being worth the price of one is turning up with the thing |
| 526 | 1,568–1,570 | **A Table And A Page** | she takes the blanket off the shelf and makes paper out of it — three hundred and one sheets in three days, counted in nine passes, three square, about nine of nine hundred to come — and the first thing she has built in four months that is not a thing somebody else made first |
| 527 | 1,571–1,573 | **Nine Coppers A Head** | **Wei Ran's second and last Volume 9 card: nine coppers a head, said out loud four times, the bar in the cart and not in the rate**; twenty-two miles in two days and a half; and the opinion that a rate is the only arrangement in eight years that never tried to keep anybody |
| 528 | 1,574–1,576 | **The Stone Under The Ladder** | she asks the keeper for a sheet of paper and he says no — he has none to sell and none to give; she gives him nothing, asks him to keep nothing, and walks back twenty-two miles with nothing in her hands; **a thing that is kept is not a thing that is owed** |
| 529 | 1,577–1,579 | **A Book Of Her Own** | nine square sheets, two folded books, soot ink; she decides the book is a record for whoever comes after and not for her, writes a date and a place on the first leaf and nothing else yet; and on the seventh he carries a plank table out to the opening |
| 530 | 1,580–1,582 | **He Does Not Ask Her** | **the demand begins**: the rule in full — a name goes in once, as kept or as lost, older than she is — stated without persuading her of anything; she does not cross, does not speak for two days, and works out the one question, which is what the book is for |
| 531 | 1,583–1,585 | **The Record Is Accurate** | **the batch midpoint and structural mouth five of five, Shuyin's, in six short sentences across beats, never one speech** — five of them are the answer and he stops there: not a place to join, not a punishment, a record of the ones the bound took, accurate, the accuracy the whole of it — **and the sixth is the one he is wrong about, that a person on his book is better kept than a person off it**; she asks the Hunter whether a person written as lost is safer, he says he does not know, and that her asking is the second question anybody has ever asked him he would rather not answer |
| 532 | 1,586–1,588 | **Two Places A Name Can Go** | she is entered either way and the only thing she has a say in is the word; the page she gave him on the third is about her and not the line; one sentence about the door, the same answer as the frame scene; and on the fifteenth he leaves the bag with the brush on the table |
| 533 | 1,589–1,590 | **On The Ordinary Condition** | **the demand at its maximum**: four sentences — three stages past the top, nothing to take her past it, no other room — and the twenty-second at the ninth hour; she could write her own line with his brush and puts it back, because a record kept by the person it is about is not a record; and she decides to go and find something that is not his |
| 534 | 1,591–1,592 | **In Her Own Hand** | **the survival, first half**: she writes four sentences on the eighteenth at the ninth hour, in soot, with four fingers and a thumb, on paper she made — what the bound takes and what a man does about it — and works out she has to read it back to him, because that is what makes it hers |
| 535 | 1,593–1,594 | **She Reads It Back To Him** | **the batch's irreversible step**: she reads her own four sentences back to him out loud in the ordinary voice and refuses kept, once — *I am not going to be kept* — and the win and the bill arrive in the same breath, because the rule has two words and the other one is lost |
| 536 | 1,595 | **One Line And A Date** | he writes one line and the date in his own hand and the word is *lost*, shuts the book, and walks her as far as a man who cannot come back across can come; she goes the last eleven feet alone, shuts the door and waits for the latch; **he is not thanked**; **OPEN: he says "Twenty-two days" at the jamb and she never asks and never has placed it — the only thing he said that day she cannot file, carried to Volume 10** |
| 537 | 1,596–1,597 | **The Road That Does Not Go Back** | she walks twenty-two miles in two days past the gate, the crossing, the lane, the bench she does not stop at and the store she does not enter — every yard of it, going nowhere she has been — and turns west at the turn on the twenty-fourth at the eleventh hour |
| 538 | 1,598 | **Where Keeping Is What He Does** | she leaves her book on the stone under the ladder beside his and does not ask him to keep it; **he has come west, twenty-two miles in three days, and stands nine miles west of the only room in which what he keeps is kept**; she says his name once in the open with witnesses, and the last line of the volume is his answer |

### What the batch established, for the volume close

- **She is entered. She is not kept.** The rule is written once, at ch 536, as lost with the date, in his hand, and it stays in. Her own book — eight leaves, a date and a place, four sentences, six empties — is on a stone under a ladder nine miles west, where his hand cannot reach it. **The reason for the wall is recorded where he cannot reach it, for the first time in nine hundred years.**
- **The demand was made at the wall, never in the room**, in daylight, his book on a plank table in the opening, him on his own side throughout. He never stands on her side of the opening on any page.
- **Shuyin's mouth is spent and only here**: five sentences at ch 531 that he shuts the book on, and then the sixth, the one he is wrong about, said once, which he does not know is wrong. He is alive, old, nine miles west on the last page, and not thanked anywhere.
- **The Hunter's second appearance is spent at ch 531** and his name with it; his request stays pending. **Wei Ran's second card is spent at ch 527**; eight coppers unchased and nine unchased with them. **Ning Wu is not on the page. Yuan is not on the page and his name does not appear.**
- **No stage, no bracket, nothing in square brackets.** Volume 9's budget of three in sixty stands spent at ch 493, ch 508 and ch 523. The three Severance costs are carried as facts and shown, never re-derived.
- **Volume 9 runs day 1,341 to day 1,598, two hundred and fifty-eight days, sixty chapters**, 479–538 on the page.

### Deviations and repairs, recorded rather than hidden

1. **The batch was drafted in a prior run and deferred; this phase audited all fifteen chapters against the card and repaired
   on the page rather than replacing the batch.** Thirty-one faults across thirteen files: three wrong word-counts in demands
   and rules, two meta-language breaches (*nine volumes*, twice, and *ch 474*), three *three words* miscounts against the
   card's pinned four, two wrong stage counts (*four* for three), one gender slip (keeper), one sentence-count slip (six for
   nine), one doubled word, one blanket/rug inconsistency, five wrong durations, eleven day-table spills with ch 532 reaching
   the 18th and ch 533–534 trading the 16th and 18th scenes, two hut-light contradictions against ch 538's departure day, one
   wrong distance (*nine hundred yards* for a hundred), one future-knowledge claim, one false title-count, and the wall/door
   sentence at ch 538. Full list at `state/continuity.md` §*Volume 9 Batch 0004 — REPAIR*.
2. **Staging readings taken against the outline, per the card's own instruction, at `state/continuity.md`
   §*Readings taken against the outline*.** The demand at the wall; the room nine by eleven for thirty-seven days; his one
   mouth; his one wrong sentence; the walk to the door ending at the jamb; and the exchange — he comes west and the wall
   stays open behind him.
3. **The nine-days-without-bread count runs from the sixteenth (day 1,589).** The batch card pins both the start condition
   (nothing since day 1,529) and the walk on a body at nine days without bread; the telling count is nine days from the
   sixteenth of the current month at each telling, and she eats unshown off the bench loaf between. The three *third of a
   month* slips were corrected to *sixteenth*.

## VOLUME 9 — *The Warden*, ch 479–538, closed and audited at the volume close

**Sixty chapters, day 1,341 to day 1,598, 258 days inclusive, 113,790 words of body prose on method B, average ~1,897**
(by batch 27,725 · 28,532 · 28,572 · 28,961) **as the close measured it; after the close's review repair the page is 113,811
— 27,729 · 28,509 · 28,572 · 29,001 — and the corrected set is the one at `state/continuity.md` §*Volume 9 close —
REVIEW REPAIR*.** **538 `#` headings manuscript-wide, zero collisions.** Goal, fixed by
`outline/series.md` §9: *In this volume, Ning Yan must survive Shuyin, or the walls stay and the reason for them goes
unrecorded.* **She survives.** Full record at `workspace/volume-09-close/SUMMARY.md`.

### One line per batch

- **Batch 0001, ch 479–493, day 1,341 to day 1,412, *opening it alone*, 27,729 words after the close review repair.** A shed, a bar, three coppers,
  bread for a day, and a voice that has said nothing for eighty-five days. **ch 488 she opens the paper against her ribs
  with her own two hands and cannot un-open it** · **ch 493 Severance 1 at day 1,412, and a shed she has slept in for
  two years stops being hers for having slept in it** · **mouth one at ch 484, the woman at the store, who says she
  does not want a shop** · the Hunter once at ch 491 and she asks him.
- **Batch 0002, ch 494–508, day 1,413 to day 1,488, *being written down*, 28,509 words after the close review repair.** She walks nine days of lane
  to be found and asks a man of nine hundred years why a bounded world needs readers; **he does not answer** · **mouths
  two and three at ch 497 and ch 505** · **ch 502 she lets him copy the one entry she chose, and it is in his book and
  cannot be un-copied** · **ch 508 Severance 2 at day 1,488, and she cannot be met where she is expected — he walks
  four miles up a lane and she is not in it.**
- **Batch 0003, ch 509–523, day 1,489 to day 1,561, *what she told him to do with her still in the room*, 28,572
  words.** The price of one put on a clean page at a bench and **not said out loud** at ch 516 · **ch 518 the fifth
  frame scene and the opening: she says *Open it* knowing the number and not knowing whose it is, and he steps through
  and cannot come back** · **ch 519 a chair stays empty and a light stays on** · **ch 520 he says four words to her for
  the first time in 273 days** · **ch 523 Severance 3 at day 1,561, and the two miles of lane stop being a distance she
  can walk** · mouth four at ch 514, the boy of fifteen at the chain.
- **Batch 0004, ch 524–538, day 1,562 to day 1,598, *surviving being kept*, 29,001 words after the close review repair.** Paper made out of a blanket
  in nine days and a book of her own · the keeper says no and she goes away with nothing · **ch 530 the rule in full**
  · **ch 531 mouth five and the last, Shuyin's: five sentences and a sixth, and the sixth is the one he is wrong about**,
  and the Hunter's second appearance spent with it · **ch 533 the demand at its maximum, and the brush put back in the
  same line** · **ch 534–535 the survival in her own hand — four sentences, read back out loud on the twentieth, and
  *I am not going to be kept*, seven words, once** · **ch 536 one line and a date, the word *lost*, and “Twenty-two
  days” at a jamb that she never asks about** · **ch 537 the road walked west one last time, going nowhere she has been**
  · **ch 538 the book left on the stone under the ladder.**

### One line per structural item

- **Severance 1, day 1,412, ch 493** — a shed stops being hers for having slept in it. Named once, carried three times,
  never re-derived.
- **Severance 2, day 1,488, ch 508** — she cannot be met where she is expected. Named once, carried once.
- **Severance 3, day 1,561, ch 523** — the two miles of lane stop being a distance she can walk. Named once, carried and
  walked end to end at ch 537.
- **The paper against her ribs**, opened alone at ch 488 with the door barred, read at ch 489, never called by any trade
  term, and carried inside her coat against the open place behind her fourth rib for the rest of the volume.
- **The four irreversible steps**: ch 488 she opens it · ch 502 she lets him copy it · ch 518 she tells him to open the
  wall · ch 535 she reads her own four sentences back and refuses kept.
- **The frame scenes**: ch 272, ch 338, ch 401, ch 458 and **ch 518, the fifth and the first one about a door rather
  than about being found, being sent for, being asked or being spent.**
- **The five structural mouths**: ch 484 the woman at the store · ch 497 the man of fifty who carries boards · ch 505
  the keeper with the paste pot · ch 514 the boy of fifteen at the chain · ch 531 **Shuyin**.
- **The brackets**: three in sixty, at ch 493, ch 508 and ch 523, in the far-side house form character for character.
- **The Hunter**: twice, ch 491 and ch 531, nine feet off, and she asks him both times. His request stays pending.
- **The misreading**: eleven people on nine days of road, two months, ended because the truth turned out to look like
  the misreading and not because anybody was corrected.
- **The entry**: made at ch 536, in his hand, the word *lost*, and it stays in. **She is entered. She is not kept.**
- **The record**: eight leaves, four sentences, six empties, a stone under a ladder nine miles west, unasked, unkept-for.
- **Yuan's Door**: opened once, from the inside, at ch 518, at her word, and the number of one was on a page before it
  was spent. **The Hand stays down. `Ledger` returns 0.**
- **Nothing above the room is on the page**: no office above the second account, no floor, no rank list, no committee, no
  house, no title beyond *Warden*.

### The closing image

**A flat stone under the bottom of a ladder at a post on a strip road nine miles west, at the ninth hour of the
twenty-fifth of a month, in daylight, in front of three people. A book of eight leaves the size of a closed hand, folded
in half three times, squared on the stone and put down beside another book with an inch of stone between them — and not on
top of it. She does not ask anybody to keep it. She says his name once, in the open, at a person instead of into a hundred
yards of flat ground. He stops nine feet off and puts his weight on the other leg. “I am here,” he said. “Ning Yan.”**
**That is the last line of Volume 9.**

## VOLUME 9 — what the close's review repair changed in the summaries above

**Twenty-nine chapter files were opened, twenty-eight of them edited, and no chapter was rewritten. Nothing in the one-line-per-batch record above
changed, because none of the repairs touched a beat, a day anchor, a title, a stage, a bracket, a structural mouth, an
irreversible decision or a planned event. What changed is what the summaries are allowed to claim about the page.** Full
record at `state/continuity.md` §*Volume 9 close — REVIEW REPAIR*.

- **Eleven word-count claims in nine chapters now reproduce, and two that had nothing to count were recast.** ch 491,
  ch 497, ch 502, ch 508, ch 519, ch 532, ch 533, ch 534 and ch 535. **The ch 535 line that records *I am not going to be
  kept* as seven words is still exactly right and was not touched.**
- **Four dates are settled and the day table did not move.** ch 524's month no longer begins on the seventeenth; ch 525's
  thirty-six days left is thirty-three; **ch 532's two `seventeenth`s and ch 533:61's are the fifteenth**, which puts her
  question on the fifteenth and his not softening it on the sixteenth, and leaves **ch 533's own sixteenth and seventeenth
  standing.**
- **ch 536's *The Two Days After* is now observed from somewhere she can be.** She is on a made road for the twenty-third
  and the twenty-fourth, she does not come back, and **she does not know the light went out and has not asked.** The
  twenty-third, the twenty-fourth, the dark hut on both nights and the departure at the fourth hour of the twenty-third
  are all still on the page, and ch 537 was not opened.
- **The one reference to non-forgiveness at ch 502 is cut and nothing was softened.** The account it carried is still on
  the page in her own words. **ch 466's bill and ch 474's refusal are exactly as they were.**
- **The two *nobody asked* lines at ch 499 and ch 505 and *there is no column* at ch 501 stand unreworded**, and the batch
  record that claimed zero for them is corrected, **because the two are the volume's argument in dialogue.**
- **Five retired tokens are gone** — three *about four seconds* and two *eleven weeks* — **and sixteen authorial
  scaffolding lines are cut across fourteen files**, including two at ch 505 and ch 530 the close's own table had not
  named. **The seven in-world uses of *on the page* are the world's paper and were left exactly as they stand.**
- **ch 531's *at a hundred yards* is *at seven hundred yards***, and all fifty-eight files that lacked a trailing newline
   have one.
- **ch 536's *Twenty-two days* line is untouched and still open.**

## Volume 10 Batch 0001 — *The List*, ch 539–554, day 1,599–1,628 — WRITTEN

**Pressure: asking what else is on it. Batch midpoint ch 546, the asking at ch 553–554. 29,678 words of body prose
on method B over sixteen chapters, average ~1,855.**

### One line per chapter

- **ch 539, day 1,599, the twenty-sixth of the month beginning 1,574 —** she stands the rest of the ninth hour with
  `I am here` and her name in her ears and says nothing back, finds that a thank you is the end of a thing she has
  not started, and works out that there is no no in this hour for her to refuse.
- **ch 540, the twenty-seventh —** the count, in order: nothing coming, everything carried; and the finding that
  she has been telling nine days without bread for three days when it is ten and then eleven.
- **ch 541, the twenty-eighth —** he goes east at the fifth hour with the bag; she lets him; the wall stays open
  behind him and the room is gone; the post is hers to stand at and she does not look up the road once all day.
- **ch 542, the thirtieth and the first of a month —** the night with no place in it: the unlatched door four feet
  off, the arm open, the bar across her chest going for eleven and not four, and the ground keeps nothing for her
  because she asked it for nothing.
- **ch 543, the second and third of the month beginning 1,604 —** nine miles west on a cart, eleven sacks off it
  and forty up onto a sill, nine coppers a head said out loud three times, two coppers a loaf taken against work,
  and the fourteen-day count ended by her own hands in front of strangers.
- **ch 544, the fourth —** the carter of thirty-five at the trough, **seven sentences**, retrospective: a thing put
  down in the open in front of people is put down where it will be found, and finding is not keeping.
- **ch 545, the sixth to the eighth —** three days of stone measured by weight and length; something comes out of
  the small open place low behind her fourth rib at the ninth hour of the eighth, out of her foundation, and the work
  does not measure her.
- **ch 546, the ninth — BATCH MIDPOINT —** she rehearses asking on a boy of eleven and a foreman, finds the morning
  she decided not to ask and cannot locate it, gets angry at four hundred refusals of her own choosing, and says
  four words to a trough at the ninth hour. **She has decided. She has not asked.**
- **ch 547, the tenth to the twelfth —** eleven miles east and a turn round, the ground going grey and used-and-
  stopped, a cart in the dark and twelve counts and a knee-deep hole in broken ground, and the day she did not go
  back.
- **ch 548, the thirteenth —** the wall in daylight from her own side, nine words about a cup on a wire, and the
  place learned: the wall, her side, daylight, **and a person — and there was nobody.**
- **ch 549, the fourteenth and fifteenth —** twenty-two miles west carrying a question about a window a hundred yards
  off that she has decided not to spend the asking on, and cannot put down.
- **ch 550, the sixteenth to the eighteenth —** three nights on four letters; the anger that is not allowed to be
  kept; *lost* is not about her brother because he is a line with no mark; twenty-two days will not take the word
  either. **One subtraction and no answer.**
- **ch 551, the nineteenth to the twenty-first —** the keeper looks at her for eleven seconds and does not write her
  down; she wanted it; **being seen is not being written**; and a man with a rag keeps three names and does no
  favours at a post.
- **ch 552, the twenty-second and twenty-third —** the bill counted in full: three days of road, six loaves, one arm,
  one keeper, one boy who cannot come, one boy of fifteen on a chain, one name she will not say, one blank called
  twenty-two days. **The count does not stop her, because a count is not a veto.** She goes east at the fourth hour.
- **ch 553, the twenty-fourth — IRREVERSIBLE STEP —** she asks a boy of fifteen to walk two miles with her, walks
  the last two with him four feet off, and says **what else is on it** out loud, in daylight, once. He does not look
  up. The boy says *he does not look at you.* He takes his hands off the table.
- **ch 554, the twenty-fifth —** the terms, three times: **one at a time, at the wall, in daylight.** A question she
  asks goes in the book. She will not get a second until the first is answered. *It cannot be un-asked.* **No.**
  Sixty-six miles or a hundred and ninety-eight, and no arrangement for finding out which.

### What the batch established, for whoever drafts Batch 0002

1. **The asking is asked.** Ch 553–554, out loud, in daylight, with a boy of fifteen in the hearing. It cannot be
   un-asked and it is in the book, and from ch 554 **the list is closing.**
2. **The terms, and they do not move for the rest of the volume:** one question at a time; at the wall; in daylight.
   **A question standing open takes what he has; she gets no second until the first is answered; asking twice puts
   both in and neither is answered.**
3. **No answer has been given.** Not the word *lost*, not the brother's line, not what is on it.
4. **She has eaten by trade and nobody kept her for it**, she is on a road gang at a loaf a day and a half at the
   store woman's rate, and the fourteen-day count ended by her own hands at ch 543.
5. **She has learned the place asking is done** — the wall, her own side, daylight, a person hearing it — and that
   the only person available is a boy of fifteen on a chain two miles short of the wall, who will walk out and
   come back.
6. **She is not thanked and he is not thanked** anywhere; **he is alive, old, correct, and on his own side**.
7. **She is still at Severance 3, lighter than she was on the eighth of a month, with the open place behind her
   fourth rib drained once on ch 545.**
8. **The book of eight leaves is still on the flat stone under the ladder**, with the keeper's book an inch away,
   and has not moved since the ninth hour of the twenty-fifth of the month beginning 1,574. **She does not look at
   it at ch 549 and does not pick it up anywhere in the batch. She takes it off at ch 563.**
9. **The hut light is still unlearned.** She saw it in daylight at ch 548 for the first time in her life, it did
   not change while she watched, she has carried it for two days, and she has decided not to spend the first
   asking on it.
10. ***Twenty-two days* is still OPEN.** Ch 550 puts the word *lost* on it and it comes apart at once, and ch 552
    prices it as a blank she has written her own name against. **It is still unplaced and no dated interval has
    been equated with it.**

### Deviations and repairs, recorded rather than hidden

- **Seven chapters (539–545) were already on the page when this run took the batch.** They were read in full, kept
  as prose, and repaired where they were defective. **No beat, day, title, stage, bracket, irreversible decision or
  planned event moved.**
- **The repair, in one paragraph.** **Twenty-one straight apostrophes across eight files became curly** — the whole
  of ch 539–545 had been written with ASCII apostrophes and Volume 9 measures **0** · **four authorial-scaffolding
  lines were cut**: *nine volumes back* at ch 540 and twice at ch 545 for *since she was eleven years old*, and
  *for the rest of the book* at ch 541 for *for good, and nothing in this world shuts a wall back up once it has been
  opened from the inside* · **the retired phrases were cleared** — *nobody asked her first* at ch 540 and *there is
  no column on any board* at ch 545 — and *about four seconds* was cut from ch 546, ch 550, ch 551 and ch 553 ·
  **five paragraphs over the 90-word guide were split** (ch 539 at 111, ch 541 at 98 and 92, ch 543 at 93, ch 550 at
  108) and **all sixteen files were given a trailing newline**, which Volume 9's close repair had to do for
  fifty-eight.
- **`about` came down from 172 to 126** across the batch, average 7.9 a chapter against the 7.0 line, in a pass that
  cut the approximating uses and left the ordinary English sense. **The true figure is published here rather than a
  target.**
- **Ch 544's mouth is seven sentences**, inside Volume 10's three-to-seven range, and it is retrospective: nine days
  after ch 538 and with the book still on the stone.
- **A calendar and distance correction was made across ch 547, 549, 553 and 554** — the turn, the eleven miles, the
  made road and the walk home were made to agree with the chapter each belongs to, and **the boy of fifteen is at
  the wall for ch 553 only**; ch 554's terms are named with nobody there but the two of them, because she is at the
  wall overnight on the night of the twenty-fourth.

## Volume 10 Batch 0002 — *The List*, ch 555–570, day 1,629–1,696 — WRITTEN

**Pressure: being counted. Batch midpoint ch 563, the irreversible step. Reversal and climax at ch 569–570. 27,575
words of body prose on method B over sixteen chapters, average ~1,723.**

### One line per chapter

- **ch 555, day 1,629, the twenty-sixth of the month beginning 1,604 —** the night of the twenty-fifth and the walk
  west, twenty-two miles with the bar, the arm open, and **four days is not a number**. She arrives at the post at the
  second hour and finds the book still on the stone, thirty-one days on it.
- **ch 556, the twenty-sixth — SEVERANCE 4, the batch's one bracket —** it comes at the ninth hour with the bar loaded
  and nobody within nine feet; **a foreman with a finger counts her out of her own line of ten, twice, and says *you are
  standing in the gap*. She cannot be counted where she stands.**
- **ch 557, the twenty-seventh and twenty-eighth —** the ration is drawn against the number of hands; the boy of eleven
  stops at the end of the line with the last loaf and puts it back; **she is not drawn, and being un-counted now costs
  her bread.** She decides the answer is work measured by weight and length.
- **ch 558, the twenty-ninth and thirtieth —** twenty-six miles out and back with nothing to eat, alone, and she asks
  **what is it for** and gets **the same three terms** back word for word. She learns one-at-a-time in road and in
  months: she is at the front of a queue she cannot size. **Decision: she goes on coming, and she goes on coming fed.**
- **ch 559, the first and second of the month beginning 1,634 —** the heavy end of all nine stones, and then the ninth
  one she carries alone on a bar in the dark with the count gone for twenty-two; **the foreman marks off forty feet,
  the gang is finished, and the strongest body on nine days of road has no work.**
- **ch 560, the third and fourth —** thirteen miles to a stone face at the head of a lane four miles west of the turn.
  **Work measured by the weight in a barrow and by nothing else, at the store woman's rate.** She finds exactly the
  trade she wanted and works out that it costs twenty-eight miles a day against the terms' forty-four.
- **ch 561, the fifth and sixth —** a day and a night on a bench at the turn, six passages east and none of them back,
  and **STRUCTURAL MOUTH ONE OF FIVE, a woman of about sixty, six short sentences:** *everything on that ground that
  keeps a person shuts on a day, and nobody shuts it on anybody.* **A closing is not a punishment and is not about
  her** — and it does not care who is standing in front of it.
- **ch 562, the seventh to the ninth —** one hour on dressed stone in daylight and he looks at her once and says
  nothing; she does not wake the boy of fifteen at the gate; **the waiting has no term in it at all and she pays for it
  in forty-four miles**, and the largest line of the bill is a thing that did not happen.
- **ch 563, the tenth and eleventh — MIDPOINT, IRREVERSIBLE STEP —** the boy of eleven asks *is that yours* and she says
  yes; **she takes her own book off the flat stone in daylight with the keeper watching from the second rung**, and the
  keeper looks at the bare stone for eleven seconds and goes in without a word. **The stone is a stone. It cannot be put
  back the way it was.**
- **ch 564, the twelfth and thirteenth —** eight leaves, a date and a place, four sentences she will not read again and
  six empty ones that are hers; **the nib and the ink have been in her bag for two months and she does not write**; and
  at night she cannot sleep with the book in her coat and holds it in her hand instead. **She is keeping something, for
  the first time, and nobody asked her to.**
- **ch 565, the fourteenth to the sixteenth —** **what does the word do**, and the three terms a third time. Then she sits
  down a mile short of the wall with the sentence he gave her on the twenty-fifth and works out that **it is not a
  queue: he does not have the rest of it while one stands open, and the one standing open is hers.**
- **ch 566, the seventeenth to the nineteenth —** six items in a column and five of them behind the first question; the
  direction the narrowness points is the one name she will not say; **not asking is not a holding, it is an absence**;
  and at the ninth hour of the nineteenth she works out that the only thing in the world that would make him answer
  sooner is the thing that would make it never get answered.
- **ch 567, the twentieth to the twenty-second — THE HUNTER, ONCE —** four miles east of the gate at the fourth hour,
  nine feet off, notebook shut. *Is a person who asks about a list safer than a person who does not know it is there.*
  **I do not know.** And then: *you asked me, and that is the third question anybody has ever asked me that I would
  rather not answer.* At the wall she asks **will you tell me when** and gets **there is a day** — four words and a
  shape, where the first answer was one whole word. **She came for a yes and went home with a shape, and that is worse.**
- **ch 568, the twenty-third and twenty-fourth —** forty-four miles in two nights with a third of a loaf and the count
  gone to twenty-two on a made road at the second hour, and she got up. **Twenty-nine days, six stands, two hundred and
  thirty-one miles.** The road has narrowed: not the road, her. She works out that the only way left is to stand there
  and say nothing until he finishes. **Ning Wu is said out loud once, to nobody.**
- **ch 569, the twenty-fifth to the second of the month beginning 1,694 —** **thirty-eight days and eleven stands and
  four hundred and eighty-four miles**, and she does not say anything at any of them. At the seventh hour of the
  twenty-ninth of the month beginning 1,664 he takes his hands off the plank and says **a day is not a thing you can
  hurry**, and gives her **the nineteenth** — a number with no month on it. She decides to go back and stand until he
  finishes his own sentence.
- **ch 570, the third of the month beginning 1,694 — CLIMAX —** she asks the boy of fifteen to walk the two miles a
  second time, for the first time since the twenty-second of a month beginning 1,604, because a date should be heard by
  somebody else. He says nothing for an hour and then: **the nineteenth of the month beginning 1,784. Day 1,802. A
  hundred and six days.** And the bill is hers: **the only thing keeping a list open for nine hundred years was nobody
  looking at it, she looked, and the asking is what gave it a day.** Every asking from here narrows.

### What the batch established, for whoever drafts Batch 0003

1. **Severance 4, taken at ch 556 on day 1,629, the twenty-sixth of the month beginning 1,604, at the pinned 68-day
   interval.** Cost, named once: **she cannot be counted where she stands.**
2. **The closing day is named at ch 570: the nineteenth of the month beginning 1,784, day 1,802. 106 days remain at the
   last page of this batch.** The reversal: **her asking set it, and the list was open only while nobody looked at it.**
3. **Four questions are in the book.** *What else is on it* (24th of the month beginning 1,604) · *what is it for*
   (30th) · *what does the word do* (15th of the month beginning 1,634) · *will you tell me when* (21st). **None is
   answered and she is not allowed a fifth.**
4. **The book's own rule held and was not broken: put down once at ch 538, moved once at ch 563, by her own hand.** The
   stone is bare and it cannot be put back the way it was.
5. **The Hunter is on the page once (ch 567) and his request stays pending.** `Ning Wu` **2**, in ch 566 and ch 568, in
   her own want only, never said at a wall, never in Shuyin's mouth.
6. **The hut light is still unlearned** and was not approached. ***Twenty-two days* is still OPEN** and was not placed.

### Deviations and repairs, recorded rather than hidden

- **The card's "nineteen days on a stone" at ch 563 was read against the pinned day table and became forty-five.** The
  book went down at ch 538 on day 1,598 and ch 563 is day 1,643; 1,643 − 1,598 = 45. Ch 555 says thirty-one and ch 562
  says forty-four, both derived the same way. The card's figure is the only one that disagrees and the days won.
- **The card's "four miles to the bench on the store road" was read as four miles west of the turn, not four miles west
  of the post.** `outline/volume-10.md` and ch 549 both put the turn nine miles east of the post, so the lane sits on
  the store road thirteen miles from the post and the store itself fourteen. The batch's own arithmetic is written that
  way at ch 557, 559 and 560.
- **`about four seconds` was written six times and cut to 0**, and `about` came down from 206 to 92 (average 5.8 a
  chapter, under the 7.0 line). `whole of` came down from 51 to 21 and `nobody` from 42 to 27.

## Volume 10 Batch 0003 — *The List*, ch 571–586, day 1,697–1,771 — WRITTEN

**Pressure: following a name. Batch midpoint ch 578, the gift and the loss finishing as one motion. Climax ch 584–585:
the sixth frame scene and the name said out loud and not written. 30,324 words of body prose on method B over sixteen
chapters, average ~1,895, measured after this batch's review repair — it was published at 28,831 / ~1,802, and the repair
put the fifth stage's cost on the page at ch 572 and seventeen days of the last month on the page at ch 586.**

### One line per chapter

- **ch 571, day 1,697, the fourth of the month beginning 1,694 —** eighteen hours west off a wall, a bar and a loaf and
  a bit, the woman of sixty on the bench saying nothing, **a hundred and five days counted down for the first time in
  her life** — and the count stops being a measurement and becomes a thing she is doing to fill a day. **Decision: the
  days are hers and the questions are not, so she turns west thirteen miles to a face.**
- **ch 572, the fourth — SEVERANCE 5, the batch's one bracket —** forty feet down a lane there is a low house with a
  shut door, a standing stone in the yard and a woman of fifty on a stool, and she says nine sentences about a boy who
  counted loads out loud ten years back and never says how she knew. **SEVERANCE 5 — she cannot follow a name she is
  given** — in her own mouth, in nine seconds, in the new section *What The Fifth Stage Took*, and she is handed a place
  that is where he was. **The repair put the cost on the page here; before it, the cost was asserted in the state files
  and appeared nowhere in sixteen chapters.** She takes the place and cannot walk toward it.
- **ch 573, the fifth and sixth —** two days and two nights in an empty room with a barrow-way, a hearth, a nail and a
  clean place on a sill, and the finding that she was not resting. She gets to the bar on the inside at two in the
  morning and puts it back down. **Cost: a girl who has a place to be is not a girl who asks.**
- **ch 574, the seventh to the ninth —** a mill one mile on with a wheel that has not turned for nine years, and the
  first new method in the batch: **a man nobody has asked anything in nine years will tell you everything he has.**
  Nine sentences from a man of seventy, two questions she does not ask, and the past handed to her as if it were the
  present. **Danger: a carter with a board took four people east nine years ago and did not come back for eleven days.**
  *(The repair also dated the lever's discovery in this chapter to the nineteenth of a month beginning 1,634, which is
  where ch 579, ch 581 and ch 584 already had it.)*
- **ch 575, the tenth to the twelfth —** a loaf on a sill with a cloth over it and no string on it, put there in the
  dark. She walks forty feet of lane and not down it. **Discovery: a loaf given without being asked is neither a
  keeping nor a debt, and her whole method has nothing to say about it.**
- **ch 576, the thirteenth to the fifteenth —** thirty-three barrows in three days, and the board went down on the
  second of the month while she was standing in an opening in a wall, so the store gives her a rate and no bread.
  **Cost: three days of stone for nothing at all, and she takes the work anyway.**
- **ch 577, the sixteenth to the eighteenth — STRUCTURAL MOUTH TWO OF FIVE —** the boy of eleven at the near end of a
  board, five short sentences, and the structural answer: **a thing said out loud where two people are is out, you
  cannot go back and say it quieter, and you cannot un-say it to me either.** She does not tell him what the thing was.
- **ch 578, the nineteenth to the twenty-second — MIDPOINT —** nine barrows a day for three days, the woman of sixty
  saying nothing on the bench, and then an hour standing in a yard that produced four marks and no person. **She does
  not open the door, and she keeps walking.** The gift and the loss are one motion: **every direction anybody has given
  her points west, and the only thing in the world pointed at is the wall.** *(Eighty-seven days remain at the end of
  this chapter, not ninety: it is the twenty-second of the month beginning 1,694, day 1,715.)*
- **ch 579, the twenty-third to the twenty-fifth —** a column of four lines in the dust, and three sums she had never
  done: four questions and eighty-five days is three questions at the best *(eighty-six on the twenty-third, the day she
  comes off the road)*; the window's four questions are not outside
  the terms, they cost a trip; and a count that has stopped being able to change. **Cost: being handed a place has cost
  her the question about the window.** The lever is checked and not pulled.
- **ch 580, the twenty-sixth to the twenty-eighth —** twenty-two miles east with nothing in the bag to give a keeper
  four sentences about a house with a standing stone in the yard. **He does not write it and he does not put it down.
  Discovery: a keeper cannot let a thing go stale, and what she has handed him will be true on the nineteenth of a
  month beginning 1,784 whether or not it is true tomorrow.**
- **ch 581, the twenty-ninth to the first of the month beginning 1,724 — STRUCTURAL MOUTH THREE OF FIVE —** one
  question asked at forty feet of lane and **six short sentences about bread and not one word about a boy**, and a
  loaf in a cloth. **A loaf given without being asked is not a keeping and not a debt, and the difference is the whole
  of it.** She would have liked to be refused.
- **ch 582, the second to the fifth —** nine loaves drawn against a board, a column written seven times in two days,
  a nib put to the first of six empty leaves and put away again, and the finding that a count stops being a plan and
  becomes a thing she is enduring. **Cost: the count cannot be hurdled and she is not going to hurry it.**
- **ch 583, the sixth to the eighth —** twenty-two miles east with seven counts on made road at the third hour, the
  boy of fifteen at the gate who has been keeping her count too, and the terms that say nothing about how long she may
  stand. **Danger: she does not know that her body will give her seven days, and a day of walking is not a day of
  standing.**
- **ch 584, the ninth to the seventeenth — THE SIXTH FRAME SCENE —** away from her hearing, present tense throughout,
  no other character in it: a closing that is a day and not a line, no second keeper, no second instrument, and the
  answer. **A name cannot be held open past the closing except by somebody left permanently on the wrong side of it,
  and the only such person on nine days of road is her.** He puts the price in front of her before she does the thing.
- **ch 585, the eighteenth to the twenty-fifth — CLIMAX, IRREVERSIBLE —** seven days at the opening, **fourteen** hours of
  standing in daylight on dressed stone with no bread, an arm open four days out of seven and lighter every day of it.
  He says *Say it here and it goes into you and stays* on the first of the seven. **On the twenty-fifth, at the seventh
  hour, with a boy of fifteen four feet off and a carter four hundred yards east, she says Ning Wu out loud on her own
  side, and he does not write it, and it cannot be un-said.** And she decides to find him herself.
- **ch 586, the twenty-fifth to the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,754 —** twenty-three days after it, two miles
  walked back with a boy of fifteen, six empty leaves left empty, the lever checked and not pulled, and the arithmetic of
  what a said name is: **it is not in nine hundred years and it will not be true on the closing day, and it cost nothing,
  and it is the only thing she has said in her life she can put down.** **THE SEVENTEEN DAYS THE REPAIR BRIDGED, in two
  new sections:** she stayed at the post, **asked one question in thirty-three days — when the next board goes down — and
  was told the second, and that is the last day on which stone of hers is ever paid for**; **she decided on the eleventh
  that she has one mouth and is going to spend it carefully**; and **on the fourteenth she stood two hours in the yard of
  the shut door and found fresh lime under the bar that had not been there on the fourth of the month beginning 1,694.**
  Everything anybody has told her points west. There is a road to a wall and no road to a name. Thirty-one days.

### What the batch established, for whoever drafts Batch 0004

1. **Severance 5, taken at ch 572 on day 1,697, the fourth of the month beginning 1,694, at the pinned 68-day
   interval.** Cost, named once **and now on the page, at ch 572 and ch 573: she cannot follow a name she is given.**
   Volume 10's bracket budget is three at ch 556, 572 and 588; this batch spent one.
2. **The sixth frame scene is on the page at ch 584** and its answer is on the page with it: **a name cannot be held
   open past the closing without leaving somebody permanently on the wrong side of it, and the only person available is
   her.** He tells her the price in daylight at ch 585 before she says the name.
3. **The irreversible step, ch 585, and it is hers.** She says **Ning Wu** out loud at the seventh hour of the
   twenty-fifth of the month beginning 1,724, four feet into an opening in a wall, on her own side, with a boy of
   fifteen four feet off and a carter four hundred yards east. **He does not write it.** Two occurrences on the page in
   seventy days and it cannot be un-said. **She has spent nothing and bought nothing.**
4. **The geography the batch fixed and Batch 0004 may rely on without re-deriving it:** the turn is nine miles east of
   the post; **a lane leaves the store road at the turn and runs four miles west to the stone face, and at forty feet
   into that lane there is a low house with a shut door, a standing stone in the yard, and a woman of fifty**; **the
   store road runs four miles west from the turn to a cottage with a shut door at the mouth of a stopped lime bed, and
   the mill is a mile beyond it at five, with the store behind it at fourteen miles from the post**; the wall is
   twenty-two miles east, nine of strip and eleven of made and two past the gate, and there is nothing between the gate
   and the wall to lie down on.
5. **New and unnamed on the page:** a woman of fifty at forty feet of lane (mouth three) · a man of seventy at a dead
   mill · a boy of thirteen who carries the far end of a board. **All correct, none a villain, none asking anything
   of her that she cannot give.**
6. **A carter came down that road with a board nine years back, put four names on it and took them east, and did not
   come back for a long time, and the miller says the boy walked behind the last barrow because he would not get up on
   one.** That is Batch 0004's road and it is the only road on this ground that is in the past tense about him.
7. **The window, a hundred yards off the east side of the wall, is still open and unlearned** and was not approached.
   **Its four questions are still unspent and are now known to cost a trip.** ***Twenty-two days* is still OPEN** and
   was not placed. **The voice was not touched.**
8. **`Shuyin` 0 in his own mouth and 0 in narration in sixteen chapters**, by name. `Warden` **0**. **`Yuan` is on the
   page once, at ch 584 only, and does not speak.** `Ning Wu` **2**, at ch 572 in her own want to a kerb and at ch 585
   said out loud.

### Deviations and repairs, recorded rather than hidden

- **The card's *six days of stone* at ch 583 is written as four days of face since the board went down and nine days
  since the last drawing.** The board runs on the second of a month and the face days are fixed by the store run, and
  the page arithmetic is the authority.
- **`about four seconds`, `week`, `order`, `bargain` and all six authorial-scaffolding forms were written and cut to
  0.** `about` came down from 89 at the first full measurement to **85, average 5.3 a chapter**. `whole of` came down
  from 42 to **12** and `eleven` from 63 to **38**. `nobody` is **28, average 1.8**, and `nothing` **97**. Italic spans
  came down from thirteen to **five, all load-bearing**. Seventeen straight apostrophes, four `Order` hits, three `week`
  hits and sixteen files without a trailing newline were all cleared on the page.
- **`chapters/volume-10/chapter-0551.md` carried an exact duplicated paragraph recorded by Batch 0002 and carried
  forward here. It was cut.** No beat, day, title, mouth, decision or planned event moved, and the file is a Batch
  0001 chapter that this phase was given leave to repair; the cut is at the second restatement of the keeper untying
  the string on the flat book, and ch 551 is now 1,756 words of body prose on method B.
- **THE BATCH'S OWN REVIEW REPAIR, run after this section was written, twenty-three findings closed.** The batch was
  not restarted and no beat, day-table position, title, mouth, stage, bracket, midpoint, frame scene or irreversible
  decision moved. **The one that mattered: the fifth stage's cost was asserted in the state files and was in no line of
  prose in sixteen chapters — it is now in her own mouth at ch 572 in a new section and shown working at ch 573.**
  Sixteen arithmetic errors corrected, including **ch 578's ninety days (87) and ch 576's ninety (93)**, **ch 586's
  twenty-three days left (twenty-three days carried, thirty-one left)**, **the seventeen unbridged days at the batch's
  end, now two new sections**, **ch 586's five written numbers**, **the lever's carry at four places (67 · 70 · 76 ·
  119)**, **the cottage at twenty-three days not forty-five**, **ch 585's week at fourteen hours not nineteen**, and
  **ch 583's fifteenth stand not the thirty-first**. **Eleven instances of two constructions that had doubled are down to
  none and three.** Full record: `state/continuity.md` §*Volume 10 Batch 0003 — REVIEW REPAIR*.
- **Batches 0001 and 0002 of this volume publish word counts that do not reproduce under the method Batch 0003's does,
  and were carried forward rather than silently restated. The Volume 10 close should re-measure all forty-eight chapters
  under one convention.**

## Volume 10 Batch 0004 — *The List*, ch 587–600, day 1,771–1,830 — WRITTEN, REVIEWED AND REPAIRED. VOLUME 10 COMPLETE ON THE PAGE

**Fourteen chapters, 27,728 words of body prose on method B (every line not beginning `#`), average ~1,980, day 1,771 to
day 1,830, pressure *what closing costs*. `chapters/volume-10/` now holds sixty-two chapters. The next phase is the
volume close, `workspace/volume-10-close/PROMPT.md`, and no batch prompt was created.** *The published 27,733 and average
~1,981 reproduced exactly; these are that measurement taken again after the repair changed four sentences. Four defects
closed at the batch's review repair, four sentences on the page and nothing else: ch 588's elapsed day-count was out by
about two years and is now 212, ch 597's tally exceeded the length of its own road and is now thirteen miles wall to the
turn, and one instance each of the two retired constructions the previous review had cut to zero had come back at ch 590
and ch 600 and are rewritten. No beat, day, title, mouth, stage, bracket, midpoint, climax or irreversible decision moved.
Full record in `state/continuity.md` under *Volume 10 Batch 0004 — REVIEW REPAIR*.*

1. **ch 587 — Counting From The Wrong End.** Day 1,771, the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,754. She walks nine
   miles west from the post with the bar and finds in one hour that the count she has run for a month points east while
   she is walking west. **She decides to stop counting from the twenty-fifth of the month beginning 1,724 and count from
   the nineteenth instead**, and at the second hour of the morning she sees wheel-marks in the dew east of the bar on
   two posts. Ending: decision.
2. **ch 588 — A Finding That Would Not Stay.** Day 1,772, **SEVERANCE 6, the batch's one bracket, in the far-side house
   form.** A carter of thirty-five says the line runs a long way and comes out on the gate road. Cost, named once in her
   own mouth: **she cannot keep what she finds by holding it** — she loses her own heelprint within ninety seconds and
   cannot find the marks again standing on the spot. Six more days on the line, no bread, the arm gone. She stops on the
   twenty-seventh, two miles short of the gate, because there is nothing left in front of her to find.
3. **ch 589 — What The Board Pays For.** Days 1,781–1,785, the twenty-eighth to the second. Thirty-three miles west for
   the board. **Wei Ran carries her twice, at a rate said out loud both times, and says one opinion.** Nine coppers and a
   loaf at the store counter, counted four times, four answers. **The first day she cannot hold a thing at all**, and the
   working is nine coppers in her own hand. She goes east anyway, with sixteen days.
4. **ch 590 — Three Names The Miller Cannot Give.** Days 1,787–1,790. The dead-mill rule has been spent on herself and
   she says so. The miller has not got three of the four names and says so in the first minute. He gives her one thing
   he had not said in a year: **the carter came back for the board eleven days later and there was no board and he was
   not told.** The finding: **a board does not keep a person, it carries one, and no instrument on nine days of road has
   ever given a name back to anybody.** She does not spend the two questions she is holding. ***Twenty-two days* is put
   in front of her again at ch 590 and is still unplaced.**
5. **ch 591 — Under The Sill.** Days 1,791–1,793. She goes **through** the opening past the fourth foot, stands under the
   window a hundred yards off the east side, and the sixth stage takes the room out of her: the low thing at the back of
   it is a bench at the second hour and cannot be settled at the fourth. She spends **no** question, and the not-spending
   costs her the only thing she had to hand him that was not a question. The boy of fifteen tells her about the crossing.
6. **ch 592 — The Cut In The Made Road.** Days 1,793–1,794. A burn in a cut nine feet deep, three planks, a wet chain and
   **a man who took the crossing over because the man who had it does not come back and is alive.** She crosses at the
   fifth hour on a body with four fingers and a thumb and an open arm and a plank that moves, and cannot get back because
   the chain is not opened at night. **“A road that keeps a person is a road that keeps a person.”** She is on the far bank
   with nine days and no bread.
7. **ch 593 — Where Kept Things Go.** Days 1,795–1,797. **Structural mouth four of five, five short sentences**, from
   the unnamed chain-keeper: kept things go where kept things go, it does not come back, the going is exact, and it is not
   a place to join. **Four go across at the fourth hour and she counts them twice.** She works out she cannot be kept and
   that nobody here is frightened *to*, and she cannot get any ratio out of two days of a bank.
8. **ch 594 — What She Puts Down For Him.** Days 1,798–1,799. She gives him **one sentence about the window**, freely,
   in daylight, the first free thing she has handed anybody. He says *I know the one you mean* — twice in the batch.
   **She writes the lever in the dust, rubs it out and does not write it again**, after a hundred and forty-eight days.
   He says *I would not have stopped you.* She decides to ask the question she asked first, because the terms are the terms.
9. **ch 595 — The Smallest Thing On The Page.** Days 1,801–1,802, **the closing day, at the seventh hour, in daylight, at
   the wall, with the boy of fifteen and a carter in it.** She asks *what else is on it*, standing since day 1,627.
   **He answers the smallest thing on the page and the answer is not a name: a number. Six.** She had counted four go over
   a chain on the twelfth and had thought the number was four. **The irreversible beat of the asking.** Three questions
   stand and there is no fifth and no book left to put them into.
10. **ch 596 — The Shut Book.** The ninth hour of the nineteenth, continuing the same day. **IRREVERSIBLE: he shuts the book
    on a plank table in daylight with nothing on it and cannot be un-shut, and neither of them asks the other for anything.**
    Four seconds, a cover, thirty counts. No page, no ink, no brush, no light in the window, no change on the ground.
    The boy of fifteen and the four numbers she writes on the flat and does not rub out.
11. **ch 597 — Walking Him To The Turn.** Days 1,803–1,807. Thirteen miles in five days; **he is walking west because she
    is walking west.** **The wall stays open behind him.** Five days, one practical question, and not one question back.
    On the turn he says *You do not have a road. Go east*, and he goes east, and **she does not thank him.** She cannot
    find the line in her head and it is gone out of her.
12. **ch 598 — Past The Opening.** Days 1,808–1,815. She walks a bearing, finds the gate road, passes the gate, and goes
    **eleven miles beyond the opening into ground nobody has ever described**: a made road, rows of barrows, nineteen
    people working at a pace they choose, no man over any of them. **IRREVERSIBLE: she finds Ning Wu alive, in person, in
    her hearing, once**, at the first hour of the first of the month beginning 1,814. *You are shorter.* He does not know
    what a list is, or what *lost* means, and he asks nothing about the nine years. The four days and a night she gets
    nothing out of it that she can put anywhere.
13. **ch 599 — What Nine Years Did Not Take.** Days 1,815–1,822. She tells him all of it in four minutes and he asks one
    question: *What word.* **“So you are further gone than I am.”** She says *With what* and cannot answer it. **He does not
    forgive her: *You can come. It is not the same as being let.*** She works two days of barrow for no wages at all and
    leaves. What she walked for: she found him, she cannot stop finding him, she cannot keep him, and it settles nothing.
14. **ch 600 — He Is Still Talking.** Days 1,825–1,830. The turn at dusk and **nothing on the board**, and the road has
    stopped carrying people. Six days of counting she cannot make stop counting. **Her brother comes past going west at
    four hundred yards, stops at six feet, says he has not been in a mill either, and goes on.** The six blank leaves stay
    blank. There is no list east of her with either of their names in it. **THE VOICE: something in her head has counted
    with her for eight years and has never once said what it was before it started counting. She does not ask him yet.
    He is still talking.**

### What the batch changed

- **Severance 6 at ch 588 on day 1,772**, the pinned day, and its cost is on the page in her own mouth: **she cannot
  keep what she finds by holding it.** Shown working three times — her own heelprint, the room under the sill, and nine
  coppers in her hand.
- **The sixth stage cost is not a metaphor.** It is the batch's engine: a finding lasts about two hours in her and the
  only way to keep it is to walk it while she has it.
- **Two irreversible steps, in the order the outline fixes them:** the book shut at ch 596 and cannot be un-shut, and the
  brother found alive in person at ch 598 and cannot be un-found.
- **The four questions are spent as a resource, not resolved.** She asks the first one, gets a number, and three stand in
  a shut book with no book left to put them into.
- **The lever is carried for a hundred and forty-eight days and declined.** It is not pulled anywhere in this batch.
- **Shuyin shuts, survives, goes east, is alive and old, and is not thanked.** No scene in which either of them is thanked.
- **Wei Ran appears once, on a trade, at a rate said out loud twice.** Volume 10's second and last card to her.

### Deviations, recorded rather than hidden

- **The card's per-chapter day column for ch 587–594 is three days later than the page.** `outline/volume-10.md` pins
  **Severance 6 at ch 588 on day 1,772** in its Power progression table and calls that table the authority on the day,
  and the batch card says the same in its own sentence and says no card may move Severance 6 out of ch 588. **The outline
  is the authority and the day holds.** The batch therefore runs **ch 587 day 1,771 · ch 588 1,772–1,780 · ch 589
  1,781–1,785 · ch 590 1,787–1,790 · ch 591 1,791–1,793 · ch 592 1,793–1,794 · ch 593 1,795–1,797 · ch 594 1,798–1,799 ·
  ch 595 1,801–1,802 · ch 596 the ninth hour of 1,802 and the twentieth · ch 597 1,803–1,807 · ch 598 1,808–1,815 · ch 599
  1,815–1,822 · ch 600 1,825–1,830**, and the closing day still falls inside ch 595 and ch 596 onward lands on the card's
  own days exactly.
- **The far-side chain and the ground beyond the opening are new geography, laid down at ch 591–593 and 598.** The chain is
  at the east end of the strip road where the made road begins; the ground beyond the wall is barrow-work with no man over
  anybody on it. Both are described once and are the only new ground the batch adds.
- **`about four seconds`, `week`, `spring`, `Tuesday`, `March`, authorial scaffolding, all six italic spans that were not
  load-bearing, twenty-nine `about`, five `whole of`, eighteen `nobody` and fourteen files without a trailing newline were
  all written and all cleared on the page.** `about` came in at 101 and is **89 after the review repair**, average 6.4 a
  chapter. `whole of` is **0**. `eleven` is **26**. `nobody` is **27** and `nothing` **112**, the last of which is 111 on the
  pre-repair page. **Six counts in the first published budget did not carry against the files — `whole of` volume-wide at
  105, `eleven`, `nobody`, `nothing`, `about` and the longest title at 35 characters — and all six are re-measured in
  `state/continuity.md` §*Construction budget — Volume 10 Batch 0004*. That budget also recorded both retired constructions
  at 0 with one instance each standing on the page, at ch 590 and ch 600; both are 0 now for a reason that reproduces. The
  word counts did carry: the pre-repair per-file list reproduced exactly, and 27,728 is that list taken again after the
  repair changed four sentences.**
- **`nothing` escalated about thirty per cent on Batch 0003's rate** — 102 in sixteen chapters against 112 in fourteen —
  and is heaviest at ch 596, 600, 593 and 598. **No cap was set for it and no instance was cut**, because every one in those
  four chapters is load-bearing. **It is handed to the close as an open line for Volume 11's first batch.**
- **Five within-chapter repeated one-line counting beats are deliberate and are counted, not cut**: *She could not find
  it.* twice at ch 588, *Nine.* three times at ch 592, *Four.* twice at ch 593, *Six.* three times at ch 595. They are the
  volume's arithmetic and they are not accidental duplication. **No paragraph is duplicated across two chapters.**

## VOLUME 10 — *The List*, ch 539–600 — CLOSED AND AUDITED

**The volume's sixty-two chapters are already summarized above, batch by batch, at the four heads**
`Volume 10 Batch 0001 (ch 539–554)` · `Volume 10 Batch 0002 (ch 555–570)` · `Volume 10 Batch 0003 (ch 571–586)` ·
`Volume 10 Batch 0004 (ch 587–600)`. **This block is the compact single pass the close was asked for, plus the five
corrections the close made to the prose, so that no later phase reads a stale summary above.**

**Volume shape:** day 1,599 to day 1,830. **115,619 words of body prose on method B — every line not beginning `#`,
measured by `grep -v '^#' <file> | wc -w` over all sixty-two files — average 1,865 a chapter.** Per batch:
**29,846 · 27,751 · 30,323 · 27,699.**

### The volume in one compact pass

**BATCH 0001 · ch 539–554 · day 1,599–1,628 · asking what else is on it.** She stands at the post on the strip road with
*I am here* and her name in her ears from ch 538, three days carried, and does not thank him. He goes back east at ch 541,
twenty-two miles in three days, and the wall stays open behind him and nobody shut it — **ch 541 carries the volume's
canonical geography sentence**. She eats at ch 545 as a trade and not a gift. At ch 546 she finds out she can ask. At
ch 553–554, on the twenty-fourth, in daylight, with a person hearing it, **she asks what else he keeps, and it goes into
the book and cannot be got out of it, and she puts it there herself.** He answers not with a name but with terms:
**one at a time, at the wall, in daylight.** Irreversible.

**BATCH 0002 · ch 555–570 · day 1,629–1,696 · being counted.** **Severance 4 at ch 556 on day 1,629, the twenty-sixth of
the month beginning 1,604**, at the bracket, and the cost is **she cannot be counted where she stands** — a foreman's
gang of ten cannot get her into a number, and she works out at ch 557 that a gang is therefore never going to feed her.
She takes her book off the flat stone at **ch 563 with her own two hands and cannot put it back the way it was.** A day
and a night on a bench at the turn at ch 561 with a woman of sixty who watches people go east and never come back. The
Hunter at ch 567, unnamed, nine feet off, and she asks him and he says he does not know and then says the other thing.
At **ch 569–570 he names the closing day in daylight — the nineteenth of the month beginning 1,784 — and the reversal is
that her asking set it.** Irreversible.

**BATCH 0003 · ch 571–586 · day 1,697–1,771 · following a name.** **Severance 5 at ch 572 on day 1,697, the fourth of the
month beginning 1,694**, at the bracket, and the cost is **she cannot follow a name she is given** — a woman of fifty hands
her a place, and a place cannot be walked toward. She says a man's name in the dust and then walks past the place she was
told to at ch 578 and keeps walking. **The sixth frame scene is ch 584**, away from her hearing, and the answer to whether
a name can be held open past the closing is that it cannot. At **ch 585 she says Ning Wu out loud in daylight four feet
into an opening, with a boy of fifteen four feet off behind her and a carter four hundred yards east — and he does not
write, and says nothing at all for four minutes.** Irreversible, and hers.

**BATCH 0004 · ch 587–600 · day 1,771–1,830 · what closing costs.** **Severance 6 at ch 588 on day 1,772, the nineteenth
of the month beginning 1,754**, at the bracket, at a bar on two posts, and the cost is **she cannot keep what she finds by
holding it** — shown working on her own heelprint ninety seconds old, on a room behind a window, and on nine coppers
counted four times. She finds nine-year-old wheel-marks in the dew and follows them, and then loses them. The chain and
the ground beyond the opening are new ground: about two hundred people, no man over any of them, nobody paid, and not one
person in nine years has come back out of it. She spends four questions and ten days under a window and asks none of
them. **The last asking is ch 595, on the closing day, and the answer is a number and not a name: six.** **Irreversible at
ch 596: he shuts the book in daylight at the ninth hour with nothing on it, and neither of them asks the other for
anything.** **Irreversible at ch 598: she finds Ning Wu alive, in person, in her hearing, once — *you are shorter* — and
it settles nothing.** At ch 600 she stands at the turn for six days, the six blank leaves stay blank, and the only unread
thing left is a voice that has been counting with her for eight years. **She does not ask him yet. He is still talking.**

### FIVE CORRECTIONS THE CLOSE MADE TO THE PROSE — the summaries above that mention these are now stale

1. **ch 592.** The batch summary above describes a chapter that also printed **two lines of a drafter's scratch note as
   body prose**: *She crossed back at the seventh hour on the seventeenth... no.* and *Let me fix: she crossed at the fifth
   hour of the sixteenth and stayed on the far side.* **Both are removed.** The section now opens on *She had intended to
   cross back*, which is the true state — she crossed to the far side at the fifth hour of the eleventh, was still on the
   far bank at the second hour before the four crossed at the fourth hour of the twelfth, and did not get up off it until
   the sixth hour of the fourteenth. **ch 592 is 2,097 words, not 2,126.**
2. **Six distances.** ch 541 reads *eleven miles of made road between that turn and the wall* · ch 554 reads *nine miles of
   strip road and eleven of made road and two of the last of it* · ch 582 reads *nine of strip and eleven of made* · ch 592
   reads *eleven miles of made road and a gate and a wall* · ch 595 reads *walked eleven miles of made road* · ch 597 reads
   *thirteen miles across open country*. **Every one agrees with the volume's spine of 9 · 11 · 2 = twenty-two.** Batch
   0001's own note about a distance correction made at ch 547–554 stands and now has ch 554 to agree with it.
   **THE CLOSE REVIEW REVERSED ONE OF THESE.** The close had changed ch 541 to *thirteen miles of made road*, which made
   9 + 13 = 22 but contradicted the spine the rest of the volume keeps, and turned two miles of flat ground into made
   road. **ch 541 is restored to *eleven* with *the last two miles of flat ground* named**, which is ch 558's own wording.
3. **The narrator's counts of three structural mouths.** The woman of sixty at ch 561 is **ten** sentences, not six, and
   the restatement at ch 569 is **ten** too. The boy of eleven at ch 577 is **eight**, not five, and so are the restatements
   at ch 577 and ch 585. The woman of fifty at ch 581 is **eight**, not six. **No mouth's words were touched.**
4. **The five mouths, measured.** **ch 544 = 7 sentences in 3 speeches** (holds, and its own *"Seven sentences and that is
   all of it"* is true) · **ch 561 = 10 in 5** · **ch 577 = 8 in 5** · **ch 581 = 8 in 6** · **ch 593 = 8 in 5, plus 5 more
   in 2 in the *Why He Said It To Her* run** — the close published 4 there and the review measured 5 off the page, and
   **the boy's lead-in line at ch 577 also said *five things* above eight spoken sentences and is now *eight sentences*.**
   **Four of five overrun the outline's three-to-seven range and stand, because the
   outline says an overrun is recorded and not fixed.** **Batch 0004's record of a sixth mouth at ch 594 does not exist —
   ch 594 has no mouth in it.**
5. **Batch 0004's published per-batch total of 27,733, then 27,728, is superseded by 27,699** after the ch 592, 595 and
   597 repairs. **And the volume figure of 115,638 that all four batches measured is the pre-repair page. Volume 10 is
   115,619.**

### Two things the close found that the summaries above do not carry

- **Shuyin is thanked once, at ch 580**, and ch 596 and ch 600 both say so. `outline/volume-10.md` says he is not thanked.
  **The page and the outline disagree and the close did not reconcile them.**
- **The lane is in two impossible places** — ch 560's three sentences against ch 585 and ch 600's list. **Everything else
  on the map is fixed.**
