## VOLUME 11 — *What He Was*

### Batch 0001 (ch 601–615) — day 1,831–1,841 — WRITTEN

**`chapters/volume-11/` holds ch 601–615, fifteen chapters, 29,284 words of body prose on method B (every line not
beginning `#`), day 1,831 to day 1,841, average 1,952 a chapter. Zero bracketed layer lines. Severance 6 throughout.
One irreversible step, at ch 609. Fifteen titles, none colliding with the 600 standing headings.**

- **ch 601, day 1,831 (the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,814), *The Bar Laid Down*.** Dawn at the turn with his
  talking in her ears. She goes through the nine things she owns at the first hour. The board on the two posts four
  hundred yards east has been bare since the second of a month beginning 1,754 and he knows the weight of the last load
  on it. **Fourteen days to the next board; eleven days to the end of standing.** She lays the bar down flat on the stones
  at the third hour and the staying is a decision, because things happen to her and standing still is the only thing she
  owns that nothing can be done to. The woman of sixty moves six inches along the bench and does not look up, for the
  first time in four months. 271 days of it. **Decision: tomorrow she goes to find out where the talking
  comes from.**
- **ch 602, day 1,832 (the nineteenth), *What The Nine Came To*.** Nine coppers on the flat of the bar: nine, nine,
  eight, nine, nine, ten. The stones in the third row of the wall by the trough hold at nineteen and the days hold at two
  hundred and seventy-two. **The count does not miss on the coppers; it misses on money, because money is the first object
  she owns that has a direction.** The only count of money ever any good to anybody is a number said out loud across a
  counter, and she says the whole of that out loud on the stones. 272 days.
- **ch 603, day 1,833 (the twentieth), *A Voice With No Address*.** Thirteen miles east. She stands in **twelve** places
  where she has heard him say something and he talks in every one of them. The gate stands open with no one on it; the
  wall stands open with no plank table in it; the opening eight feet high has nothing in it but her. Quarter of a mile
  past the flat she asks *are you here* and he says **I am not anywhere.** She walks thirteen miles **without counting one
  figure** for the first time since the eighteenth of a month beginning 1,814, and works out that she spent the count on a
  voice. On the flat past the wall the count she cannot start produces **twenty-two** with no unit on it, and it goes when
  she stands. 272 days, carried.
- **ch 604, day 1,834 (the twenty-first), *The Mile She Did Not Walk*.** Four miles west to the cottage with the shut door at
  the stopped lime bed. She does not touch the bar on the door. She looks for six things a boy of nineteen leaving at dusk
  would have left and finds none, because he did not stop, and he is going a mile further on to a mill with a man of
  seventy in it. **She turns round at the shut door, one mile short of him, because going to the mill would be following
  him.** Four miles back with the arm gone from the first mile, the bar down in the road for ten minutes, and the bar back
  on at the turn with both hands. 274 days.
- **ch 605, day 1,835 (the twenty-second), *The Loaf And The Number*.** Five miles west to the store behind the mill. The
  woman behind the counter counts nine in a second and a half and says it, and for the first time in ten years a figure is
  in two places. **Bread at two coppers a loaf, said out loud, put down without being counted.** No thank you; no question;
  no mother's name. **Ning Wu walks west past her at the eighth hour with a mill-sack on a strap, says *you have eaten* and
  *that is a trade*, and goes on.** Five sentences in nine years and three of them are of bread. The bill for turning round
  is the whole of it. 275 days. Seven coppers left.
- **ch 606, day 1,836 (the twenty-third), *The Kind Of Person Who Asks*. MIDPOINT.** She counts every question she has ever
  put to him — eight of them — and they all want a figure, a day, a yes, a no or a word with a meaning, and **every
  one of them had somewhere to go.** The question she has carried since the fifteenth of the month beginning 1,634 does not, and there is no
  book, no column, no board and no wall left to put it in. What she works out at the fifth hour is that **she has been the
  filing for eight years** and not one answer was ever in a book. The lever is taken out, turned over, and put away: a
  thing you can ask twice is a thing you can be refused twice. **DECISION, said out loud on the bench: *I can ask him.*** And
  the second half: she will not do it with nothing in her hand.
- **ch 607, day 1,836 (the twenty-third), *A Rate With An End In It*.** **Wei Ran's last rate on this ground: six coppers
  the four miles, said out loud going out and said out loud coming back, the bar in the cart and not on the seat.** One
  carrying, one opinion, no thank you, no question either way, no romance. Wei Ran: *there is no rate after this one and
  no man west of this turn who would carry a bar for money.* She spends six of seven coppers and counts what is left twice
  and gets two, then one. **One copper, a quarter of a loaf, a bar, a bag, six blank leaves.** He points out that she has
  counted what is left of her eleven times that day and has never once counted what is left of him.
- **ch 608, day 1,837 (the twenty-fourth), *What A Furnace Uses*. FIRST ORIGIN MOUTH.** A man of forty-five comes west on
  foot with his hands burned into the creases and stops at the turn, and she reads his hands at eight feet the way she has
  read hands since she was six. She asks about the lime bed four miles west and he worked it for a year. **The mouth, in six
  sentences across the chapter: the boy weighed them before they went in · nobody pays him, his father fires · a boy who
  counts what nobody is paying him to count is a boy the furnace will use · it is not the same thing as keeping and not one
  man at a fire thinks it is, and the only people who found the difference out were the ones it was done to · it happened
  without anybody meaning it and not one man there was cruel · he had a name and nobody at that fire used it.** He asks her
  the only question anybody has asked her: *what do you count.* **She turns her own hand over and it is the same hand.** He
  tells her he has told this to two people in twenty years and she is the third.
- **ch 609, day 1,837 (the twenty-fourth), *Before You Started Counting*. IRREVERSIBLE. THE BATCH CLIMAX.** She carries the
  bar a hundred yards into the broken ground, checks there is not a soul within four hundred yards, and at the ninth hour,
  alone, in the ordinary voice, **she asks: *What were you before you started counting.*** **He does not answer. He goes
  careful.** He asks her to say it again, and *I want it on the record that I asked.* Then he names the terms: **one at a
  time. in daylight.** Four minutes of silence she counts. She works out that the wall is gone, so a question is done
  standing: **eleven days each way to nowhere.** She pulls the lever open in the dark and does not pull it. The terms are
  said out loud so two people hold them, and they are not written in the dust. **It cannot be un-asked, and the second one
  who has the words is him.**
- **ch 610, day 1,838 (the twenty-fifth), *Waiting With No Road In It*.** Nothing is different. He says the number of
  stones in the third row of the wall by the trough and she counts them. The waiting has no road in it: **the new third term
  is standing.** The woman of sixty does not move along the bench today, and being noticed once is worse than not being
  noticed. She prices the exposure by standing on the stones saying figures aloud until a carter comes through, and the
  carter says *good* and goes on. She does not put the six sentences on the second leaf. 278 days. **Decision:
  not tomorrow — she has three mornings and she is going to find out what a fire is on foot first.**
- **ch 611, day 1,839 (the twenty-sixth), *The Best Day She Has Had*.** The stone face, four miles west, six hours to nine,
  measured by weight and not by hands, a man with a chalk on a string. **She puts up more stone in three hours than in any
  day in two years and does not stop at the ninth hour.** The little open place behind her fourth rib goes quiet and then
  does not; ten seconds; no sound in it; **what it took came out of the ground under her feet and not out of her hands.**
  He says nothing about it, as he has said nothing through all nine. **Cost: she says the figure back to herself four times
  and gets eleven, eleven, twelve, eleven. She will be able to say she worked. She will not be able to say how much.** Worth
  eleven coppers she cannot count, six days, and the use of an arm. 279 days.
- **ch 612, day 1,840 (the twenty-seventh), *The Two Dates*.** Nine years back from today is **the twenty-seventh of
  the month beginning 1,805**, and three separate things stopped on that one date: the lime, the wheel, and a boy of ten
  going east behind the last barrow. **The fourth thing is hers and it is nine days earlier** — the small open place in her,
  the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544 — and *nine years is not a length on this ground, it is two dates nine days
  apart*. **At the fifth hour and forty minutes the woman of
  sixty turns her head about a foot and looks at the place behind her fourth rib through the front of the coat, and knows
  where to look, and holds it four seconds.** Ning Yan does not ask her one question and has gone over every hour since.
  **For nine years she has been looked for and never once looked at; and this is what looking at feels like.** He admits he
  knew at the fourth hour and did not tell her. 280 days.
- **ch 613, days 1,840–1,841 (the twenty-seventh into the twenty-eighth), *The Count That Held Twice*.** The book of eight
  leaves out on a kerb in the dark. **Six, six, six, six, six — the first count in ten years that has never come back
  different**, and the reason is not difficulty: money leaves, work leaves, days leave, and **a leaf does not go anywhere
  when you count it.** Room has never been the problem. The second leaf is four sentences she read out loud once, and it
  was never for anybody. She says every one of the six sentences, the date, the barrows, the four miles and the number she
  has not got out loud on a kerb, **and puts the nib near none of the six leaves.** What six blank leaves are: the only
  thing she owns that cannot be taken, and she has been guarding them for four months with nothing to guard. 2 hundred and
  80 days.
- **ch 614, day 1,841 (the twenty-eighth), *Asking Twice*.** She rules out five questions on the stones at the fifth hour.
  What is owed is not the harvest and not the name and not why there is a hole in her chest: **a person who has been in your
  head for eight years owes you one ordinary day of his life.** **The lever is taken out in the open at the sixth hour and
  not pulled** — a thing you can ask twice is a thing you can be refused twice, and on the ninth hour of the twenty-fourth he
  told her he does not answer a thing twice. She says out loud that she is not going to go back and do any of it better. She
  says the two terms again on the stones and does not write them. She is not frightened any more; she is flat, and she wants
  very much to be finished.
- **ch 615, day 1,841 (the twenty-eighth), *Six Hundred And Forty*. CLOSING REVELATION.** Seventh hour, daylight, a hundred
  yards off the road in the broken ground, the turn for a witness and a woman of sixty thirty feet off. **She does not say
  the words again. She asks the small one inside them: *What did you do on an ordinary day.*** He answers in four seconds,
  in the present tense, and gives her **three things where she asked for one: *Bricks. Two hundred and forty courses in a day
  at six hundred and forty to a course. I was the one who counted them. I have been counting bricks since I was eleven
  years old and I have never once been paid for it.*** A furnace east of here, not this sect. **And nineteen — and he does not
  help her decide which of the two sentences she believed.** She works out that a thing in her head that hands over whole
  entries is handing over entries and not sentences, and that he has done it since she was eight. **She does not take a
  fourth thing. One at a time.** A carter comes through at the seventh hour and twenty minutes and goes on east. And she puts
  the two together: **a boy at a fire counting what nobody pays him to count, and a man at a fire counting the same thing at
  two hundred and forty courses a day with nobody paying him.** 281 days. **She has a place to walk to.**

### What Batch 0001 leaves Batch 0002

The asking stands and cannot be un-asked. The terms stand and do not move. Nine coppers became seven, then six, then
**one**. Six blank leaves stay blank. The lever is carried and declined twice and is not pulled. A furnace east of
everything is named and not walked to. **Twenty-two** came back once with no unit on it and is still unplaced. The
window, the lime and the hut light are untouched. Severance 7 is ch 616 on day 1,842 and this batch takes no bracket.

## VOLUME 11 BATCH 0002 — ch 616–630, day 1,842–1,916

- **ch 616, day 1,842 (the twenty-ninth of the month beginning 1,814), *The Only Thing That Holds*. SEVERANCE 7.**
  Sixth hour, in broken ground a hundred yards north of the turn. **The batch's one bracket line**, in the far-side
  house form, ten seconds, no sound in it, the sound goes into the ground, and what it took came out of the ground
  under her feet. **THE COST, NAMED ONCE: she cannot hear what she is told about him — a voice counts and a book
  writes and neither one carries a boy.** He tells her a true thing about a course of low wall and she can say it
  back word for word and there is no place for it. **She forfeits the board on the second of the month beginning
  1,844** and walks east at the ninth hour with one copper she cannot count. 282 days.
- **ch 617, day 1,846–1,850 (third to seventh of the month beginning 1,844), *She Heard Every Word Of It*. DISCOVERY.**
  A widow of forty-five walking west with lime; hands burnt into the creases, read at eight feet. **She says the
  name — Yuan Sheng — for the first time in nineteen years, on a made road, at the sixth hour. It misses.** She can
  produce it perfectly and it does not arrive, and the finding is that this is not a memory and the road is the same.
  A shut book on a shelf, written by a good hand, nineteen years shut. Eleven miles of made road and two of stone.
- **ch 618, day 1,851–1,855 (eighth to twelfth), *She Did Not Go In*. DECISION.** The gate, **two miles with nothing
  on them to lie down on**, the wall and the opening standing open, the flat. **The rows: about two hundred people,
  a stone at the head of each, counted to sixty-one, and she does not go in and does not call out and does not say
  his name.** She walks on feet and not on a telling, and the air changes. 293 days.
- **ch 619, day 1,856–1,860 (thirteenth to seventeenth), *He Does Not Know*. REVELATION.** The man with the shut
  notebook, nine feet, two hours of a slope, no question asked her. **She asks whether a person who knows what a
  voice was is safer. He says he does not know, and then says that it is the fourth question anybody has asked him
  that he would rather not answer**, and one thing on the way down: a true thing is not the same as a thing that is
  yours. She never puts the name in his hearing.
- **ch 620, day 1,861–1,866 (eighteenth to twenty-third), *A Fire She Cannot Put Up*. DANGER.** Ten days on a furnace
  ground that does not want her and lets her stay. **A boy of fifteen calls every figure out loud, the way she does.**
  **The furnace will not light for her** and a boy brings her the coarse charcoal and the bark and it goes up. **She
  goes down on her hands and knees in the ash at the furnace mouth with a bar of fire four feet from her head, at
  nine counts of breath, and counts her way back to four.** The woman in the low house watches all of it and does not
  go in and does not shut the door.
- **ch 621, day 1,867–1,872 (twenty-fourth to twenty-ninth), *What She Said To A Woman With A Book*. COST.** **She is
  refused the book, truly, without cruelty**, four reasons and the last of them the one about a person who cannot
  hold a name. **And she says the cruelest sentence of her life on the woman's own step: nineteen years and you have
  never once opened it to find out whether it is true.** The woman takes it as the second and not the first and says
  it is the first one that was any use. A board on the wall in the low, and four days to it. 312 days.
- **ch 622, day 1,873–1,878 (thirtieth of the month beginning 1,844 to the fifth of the month beginning 1,874),
  *The Woman Who Opened A Book*. REVELATION. SECOND ORIGIN MOUTH.** The book comes off the shelf. **Six short
  sentences across the chapter, in five speeches, none of them a speech:** they wrote it down the same week and they
  wrote it down true · my husband did not write the order and did not carry it out · the boy was nineteen and was the
  son of the man who keeps the fire · it did not save my husband and it did not save the boy · not one person on that
  ground has ever said it was going to. **Nothing proves the crews wrong** and there is not one word in the book
  about why. **She is not going to say the name twice.** 318 days.
- **ch 623, day 1,879–1,884 (sixth to eleventh of the month beginning 1,874), *The Rule She Wrote In The Ash*.
  DECISION.** Three days and a scrape of paper: **she writes six and five comes out.** Her hand writes what she means
  at the moment it goes down. **The three things she was frightened of, and the third is the real one — she cannot
  keep a read past any better than a told one.** One whole day on holding a page and writing with one arm, a borrowed
  board and a stone. Six leaves counted once. 324 days.
- **ch 624, day 1,885–1,890 (twelfth to seventeenth), *What Her Hand Went Down On*. IRREVERSIBLE.** She stands at a
  table four minutes and does not touch a page in a good hand dry nineteen years. She reads it. **The date, the
  place, and then the name in capitals, four fingers and a thumb, on the third leaf of a blanket book.** Blanket does
  not take ink back and there is no way to put it back un-written. **She reads the date out loud at a wall later and it
  does not reach him either.** Six leaves now five. The woman asks whether it said the same one. 325 days.
- **ch 625, day 1,891–1,895 (eighteenth to twenty-second), *What Does Not Go Away*. EMOTIONAL CONSEQUENCE.** She puts
  her hand on the book before her eyes are open and the name is where it is. **Five leaves. She goes looking for the
  regret and it is not there. She makes a rule and does not say the name out loud for five days so that the road does
  not get it back. And the ground starts going quiet on her and she does not notice for six days.** 331 days.
- **ch 626, day 1,896–1,900 (twenty-third to twenty-seventh), *A Trade And Not A Kindness*. COST.** **She asks for
  work out loud for the first time in ten years**, at the long window, and it is a fifth of a man's rate and the same
  board. **Fourteen barrows of pit stone on the third of her arm and she does not stop.** She says the figure back
  and gets eleven, eleven, eleven and once twelve. **He tells her about ninety-one courses on a kiln wall and she
  does not check it and keeps on pulling.** The second barrow goes on and off and then stops. 337 days.
- **ch 627, day 1,901–1,905 (twenty-eighth and twenty-ninth of the month beginning 1,874 to the first and second of
  the month beginning 1,904), *He Does Not Have The Day*. REVELATION.** The board goes down and comes back on a cart
  and there are eight coppers on the table in a cloth and nine in her bag. **She asks what the day was before he
  started counting.** He gives three: a senior who had been at it nine years, nineteen, a morning. **She reads him
  the date off her own leaf in daylight and there is nothing on the other side of it — not a silence, not a refusal,
  not carefulness. He has never in his life had the day.**
- **ch 628, day 1,906–1,910 (third to seventh of the month beginning 1,904), *The Count Holds*. DISCOVERY.** Sixty-five
  days from the turn, forty-nine of them on this ground, fourteen on her feet and two at the turn · nine coppers
  (nine, nine, eight, nine, four times), **two hundred and forty courses at six hundred and forty
  to a course is a hundred and fifty-three thousand six hundred bricks**, three hundred and forty-six days of it and
  six thousand nine hundred and thirty-five days of him. **The test she sets herself: six figures come back, six
  sentences do not.** The count is the only instrument she has and it works on everything except a person. 346 days.
- **ch 629, day 1,912–1,916 (ninth to thirteenth), *A Yard With A Fire In It*. DANGER.** Four days on that ground
  without one word from one person in it, and she counts when the ground went quiet on her. **THE BAR OF FIRE:** **A bar of fire comes down where she was standing because she did not hear a
  man shouting from twenty feet.** She asks at the doorstep with the only reason she has, the woman asks her a
  question, and the book is turned and the woman goes out to the yard. Ten feet of light and a page.
- **ch 630, day 1,916 (the thirteenth of the month beginning 1,904), *What The Page Assumed*.
  VOLUME MIDPOINT. REVERSAL.** **She reads the harvest and it is true: a senior at the first hour, stalled before the
  second, filled with a person, a boy of nineteen off the fire, taken at the sixth hour, two weights that are not the
  same and the difference written down with no word beside it.** **He says the part that is not on the page — the fire
  did not take all of it, and the part that woke up is the part that survived. That is the first time in his life he
  has said it.** **And it does not hold her: every figure comes back and the morning does not, because she was not in
  it.** **THE BILL IS HERS AND IT IS COUNTED:** the second barrow on the twenty-first, off on the twenty-second, on
  three days, off for good from the twenty-eighth; the bowl stops on the second of the month beginning 1,904; four
  days running of not one word. **Her asking set the knowing and the past was findable only while nobody asked after
  it.** **And the page has no place on it — a book like that assumes everybody who reads it is already standing in
  the room — and she has been on that ground fifty-nine days and has not found it.** 356 days.

### What Batch 0002 leaves Batch 0003

Severance 7 held. **She cannot hear what she is told about him, and it does not work one way: a page she read herself
did not reach him either.** The name is on a leaf in her own hand and cannot be un-written; five blank leaves. One
copper spent and nine in the bag she cannot count. Sixty-eight days since she was told the name, fifty-nine of them on this ground, and no rate on any road behind her.
A furnace ground that let her stay and has gone quiet on her. A widow who has said a name out loud twice in nineteen
years and will not open the book a third time without a reason better than the last. **The harvest is named and does
not hold her, and the bill for the knowing is hers, and she is still going.**

## Volume 11, Batch 0003 (ch 631–645) — *What He Was* — day 1,916–1,996

Pressure: **knowing**. Fifteen chapters, 29,966 words of body prose on method B, average 1,997. One bracketed layer
line, at ch 632. Severance 7 throughout until ch 632, Severance 8 from it. The batch's irreversible step is ch 645.

- **ch 631, day 1,916–1,917 (thirteenth to fourteenth of the month beginning 1,904), *Seventy-Five Days On A Wall*.
  DECISION.** She will stand the day the eighth stage comes rather than be asleep for it, because she wants to be on
  the ground when she has it. **The intervals: seventy, seventy-five, eighty, and she writes the eighty in the ash and
  does not rub it out.** She walks the stone line in the dark and gets ninety-four paces the long side and fifty-eight
  the short. She opens her book to the fourth leaf, decides she will write the place there if she gets one, and then
  decides she does not want to. **She counts the ground: not one person in sixty days has asked her one question about
  her, and that is the thing she had been waiting for.** She names the day and the person. 357 days.
- **ch 632, day 1,917–1,922 (fourteenth to nineteenth of the month beginning 1,904), *A Permission That Does Not
  Hold*. SEVERANCE 8. THE BATCH'S ONE BRACKET.** Ten seconds, heavier than hungry, out of the ground under her feet,
  and no sound. **She asks him whether the permission is still binding and he says it had force and does not have
  force now and he did not do it. THE COST, NAMED ONCE: she cannot keep him by asking.** She writes the asking on the
  back of a board, and by the ninth hour she can read her own line and cannot produce the question. He asks her a
  question about her work and she finds out she was waiting all along to be asked. Five true answers and no proof she
  asked for one of them. 362 days.
- **ch 633, day 1,922–1,928 (nineteenth to twenty-fifth of the month beginning 1,904), *Six Days Of One Question*.
  COST.** She sets herself a controlled test — five questions written down, one a day, in the terms — and at the end
  of it she has five answers and not one of them attached to a moment when she opened her mouth. **Asking a person
  still works and she proves it with the boy of fifteen and with the woman of forty-five.** She is frightened, and she
  says so. The man of fifty-one names a person: the Accountant, seventy-one, at the north end, and go at the seventh hour.
- **ch 634, day 1,929–1,934 (twenty-sixth to thirtieth of the month beginning 1,904 and the first of the month
  beginning 1,934), *Nine Paces By Six*. DISCOVERY.** **THE HANDING.** The Accountant will not open the book and walks
  her onto the kiln floor instead: nine paces by six, four courses of stone from the bank at the east, laid in the week
  of the morning. **The floor has one way on to it — off the east slope, four paces down, nine paces along the north
  side — and that is why a page written by somebody standing there did not have to say where.** She kneels and finds a
  gap the width of a nail in the whole of the north joint: the stone was cut for it. **And in the same motion the
  asking goes entirely, and she says so: holding was the knowing and she had been using asking as a second way in.**
  She does not write it down anywhere.
- **ch 635, day 1,935–1,940 (second to seventh of the month beginning 1,934), *She Walks It Herself*. DECISION.** Four
  days of not going near the patch, and she catches herself walking over to look at a join in some stone four times in
  a day. **The man of fifty-one offers to walk it round with her and she says no in four parts, and the fourth part is
  the true one: she wants to have gone there.** She gets the route from the Accountant at twenty feet, times it, and
  names the twenty-first for the walk. **And she says she is not going to stop on the nine paces, because a place is
  the one thing in the world you can walk off.**
- **ch 636, day 1,941–1,947 (eighth to fourteenth of the month beginning 1,934), *Two Hands, Nineteen Years Apart*.
  THE THIRD ORIGIN MOUTH.** **A man of seventy-one opens the ground's harvest book, seven short sentences across four
  days, and the new thing is that a name written where somebody has to look at it is kept whether or not anybody reads
  it, and the keeping is accurate, and the accuracy is all of it.** She counts six of the seven back the next day; the
  one she cannot count is the one he said with the book shut, and being unable to keep it does not matter because he
  has just told her she was never going to have to. **He wrote YUAN SHENG in a good hand nineteen years ago and it is
  the same four strokes she wrote in capitals on her own third leaf.** Two hands, nineteen years apart, and she can be
  lost and the record cannot.
- **ch 637, day 1,948–1,953 (fifteenth to twentieth of the month beginning 1,934), *Carrying It East*. COST.** **The
  batch's one settlement, on listed work, at the seventh hour and forty minutes of the sixteenth, on a day of
  thirty-one lengths: ten seconds, out of the ground, and the woman of forty-five comes out on her step and asks her
  whether she goes down.** She argues with a thing that cannot happen and finds the real trouble is that there are now
  two copies and no instrument for holding them. She walks out of the east gate three hours for three days and does the
  whole list of what she is carrying, oldest first, **and finds at the bottom of it his name, in two hands. The hard
  part is not that a person can be lost. The hard part is that there is no putting it down.**
- **ch 638, day 1,954–1,960 (twenty-first to twenty-seventh of the month beginning 1,934), *Past The Patch Of New
  Stone*. MIDPOINT. REVERSAL. THE PHYSICAL CARD.** Six days of not sleeping, and then the walk: bar at the wall, bag at
  the gate, nothing in her hands. Four paces down off the east slope, nine paces along the north side, the nine by six
  on her left and four feet off it, **and she does not stop and does not slow down.** A hundred and thirty-one more
  paces to the west end, twenty seconds at the hundredth with her face down against the wall, and the mouth, and nine
  minutes at eight feet off it with the heat coming across the open air into her chest. **And then she comes back down
  the other side, past the patch at eleven feet, and out of the gate, and says out loud on the flat that she has got a
  place and she does not want it, and that she has been walking across the top of his day and coming off the far end of
  it and is good at it.** 394 days.
- **ch 639, day 1,961–1,966 (twenty-eighth to thirtieth of the month beginning 1,934 and the first to third of the
  month beginning 1,964), *The Man With Ash On His Arms*. COST.** Two hours and forty minutes of hot stone cost her
  four days of work: nine lengths, eleven lengths, sitting down twice. **She is on the ground from the twenty-second and
  cannot get up off it until the twenty-eighth, and her breath does not come back to four counts until the second of
  the month beginning 1,964, which is eleven days, not four.** She goes down on one knee in the grit in front of
  a trough in front of nine people and works out that she is not doing it again, and that the reason the nine said
  nothing about it is that they have been right the whole time. **She finds the tenth man: fifty, ash on both forearms
  from the wrist up, nineteen years of it, the one who pushes the barrow out.** She asks him one question at the mouth
  end. He turns round, takes his bar, and works the second length and the third and does not say one word. **And she
  cannot follow him, because at that hour she could walk nine paces of a floor and get back off it.** 405 days.
- **ch 640, day 1,967–1,972 (fourth to ninth of the month beginning 1,964), *Second Hour, Nineteen Years Ago*. THE
  FOURTH ORIGIN MOUTH.** He comes to the gate because she did not follow him. **Six short sentences over three days,
  and the new thing is that a thing taken out in the morning is gone where taken things go, and the going is not
  watched, and the not-watching is all of it.** She walks a mile and a half down the east track to the ash heap and
  finds nine paces by six at the top of it and says out loud that the two are not the same and that she will not make
  them the same because it would be a better story. **And she gets the morning: not the hour it was taken in, which
  belonged to him, but the hour after, an hour and a mile and a half long, with a man of thirty-one in it who did not
  look down — the first morning anybody has handed her about that ground.** And she says out loud, once, that nothing
  on this ground watched, and that is a different sentence from the one she cannot prove. 410 days.
- **ch 641, day 1,973–1,978 (tenth to fifteenth of the month beginning 1,964), *The Third Thing He Owes*.
  REVELATION.** **She asks what the tax was for, in the terms, standing, in daylight, in front of four people — and he
  says she asked a what, not a why, and gives her the shape instead, six pieces over two days: counted in days and not
  in money, one out of twenty, the same on every ground, it arrives, it comes off the board before the board goes up,
  and no person has ever been told their own total.** The woman of forty-five tells her he paid it for nine years and
  never told her what a day was for and she never asked. **And the figure off the board at ch 626 comes back the
  second time: she reads it and holds it.** 418 days.
- **ch 642, day 1,979–1,984 (sixteenth to twenty-first of the month beginning 1,964), *One In Twenty*. DISCOVERY.**
  Four counts in a row. **Days: four hundred and twenty divided by twenty comes out even, at twenty-one, and she has
  been carrying a fifth of herself out of the world since she was a child.** Coppers: a fifth of a copper, and the only
  instrument that could show it to her is nine pieces of metal that will not count. Ash: twenty heaps she makes
  identical and then has to admit proves nothing. Nineteen years: six thousand nine hundred and thirty-five into twenty
  is three hundred and forty-six and a bit, and that is a shape. **And she says it out loud, on a floor, at the eighth
  hour: it is designed. There is no join in it anywhere.** She tells the boy of fifteen at the stack so that he is not
  the only one standing in front of a fraction at nineteen. 422 days.
- **ch 643, day 1,985–1,990 (twenty-second to twenty-seventh of the month beginning 1,964), *Rubbed Out Again*.
  DECISION.** **THE LEVER DECLINED, the third time.** Asking twice would get him to the next answer sooner and would
  lose the first — **and after the eighth stage it costs her both, because two answers cannot be ordered and she proved
  that in front of a man with a board.** She writes the two lines in the ash and rubs them out and leaves the black.
  **She does not write it on a leaf: four of her five blank leaves are still blank and so is the fifth, because a line
  in her own hand that she cannot remember putting there is a thing she cannot tell apart from a thing she did on
  purpose.** She tells him in advance that he is to have no part in the day she says it, so that he cannot be the reason
  for any of it.
- **ch 644, day 1,991–1,994 (twenty-eighth to thirtieth of the month beginning 1,964 and the first of the month
  beginning 1,994), *The Price Of Being Known*. THE SEVENTH FRAME SCENE, away from her hearing, present tense
  throughout.** He reads the four days: the walk, the man of fifty, the shape with a fraction in it, and the sentence
  *it is designed*, worked out on her own with a stick in the grit. **He has been holding his question upside down for
  four months — knowing was never what used a fragment up; filing was. A name can be known without being used up, and
  that is the answer, and it costs nothing.** He looks at the thing he is a fragment of — the **Scribe**, named once,
  never speaking, never offering, never adjudicated — and at the person in the middle of the machinery who was counted
  too and told nothing. **He decides to stop being an entry, and to put it in front of her in daylight, as a person
  handing a thing over, two days before she says her sentence in the yard, so that what she says in the open is a
  thing she said to a man and not to a fragment.**
- **ch 645, day 1,995–1,996 (second and third of the month beginning 1,994), *What She Said In The Yard*.
  IRREVERSIBLE. DECISION.** **He puts it in front of her at the gate in three sentences and refuses a fourth, which is a
  name.** She tells him in advance, in the yard, in front of people, what she is going to say and that it is about her
  and not about the four people on that ground who have been exactly right. **AND THEN SHE SAYS IT, once, standing, in
  daylight, with thirteen people inside a hundred yards that she did not ask to be there: the tax is designed, it was
  made to a figure before there was a ground to put it on, it is one in twenty on every ground, it has never changed,
  no person can change it, it is counted in days and not in money — and it is for finding people like her.** Nine
  seconds. He says not one word, as he said in advance he would. The man of fifty-one puts the nib down and asks what
  she wants him to do and she tells him to go back to the board. **And she says the rest of it: she is staying, she is
  not going anywhere, she knows what it is for and she cannot un-know it and cannot un-say it, and she has walked out
  of four places in ten years and is not going to walk out of the fifth.** 436 days.

### What Batch 0003 leaves Batch 0004

Severance 8 held at day 1,917 and its cost named once. **She cannot keep him by asking** — he is findable by knowing
and not by asking, and the asking will not come back. **The tax has a shape and the shape is designed, and she has said
the purpose out loud in a yard and cannot un-say it.** A second accurate copy of his name is in a good hand in a cloth
book at the north end of a furnace ground and does not need her to be alive. A place exists — nine paces by six, one way
on — and she has walked it and past it and does not want it. A man of fifty knows what went out of an east gate at the
second hour on the morning after and has said so, and what was in the barrow is still not known and cannot be proved.
Her five blank leaves are five blank leaves. The lever is carried, not pulled, and not written. **Nothing is forgiven
and nothing is thanked.** The eighth frame scene is on the page and he has put three sentences in front of her and
refused a fourth. **Day 1,997, the fourth of the month beginning 1,994, is SEVERANCE 9 and one day off.**

## Volume 11, Batch 0004 (ch 646–660) — *What He Was* — day 1,997–2,060 — VOLUME 11 CLOSED ON THE PAGE

- **ch 646, day 1,997 (the fourth of the month beginning 1,994), *What Eighty Days Comes To*. DECISION.** She counts
  the interval out loud in the grit at the foot of the stack — seventy, seventy-five, eighty — and gets to the thing
  she has not let herself look at: **the gaps are getting longer**, and nobody has ever told her the spacing is for.
  **She asks the man of fifty-one for a line in his hand about a thing that has not happened yet**, and he writes it
  square at the top of the board with the day against it and nothing under it: *Stage taken. Tenth hour of the fourth.
  North end of the yard, in the open, ground looking on. A person present who can say so.* **She chooses the tenth hour
  so that the stage cannot take the seventh, which is the hour she gives to questions.** 437 days.
- **ch 647, day 1,997, *A Tower With A Light In It*. DECISION.** She walks out of the east gate with nothing in her
  hands and works out that every figure she has about him is in a head nineteen years old. The tower with a light in it
  arrives — a hauler's report from five years back, a tower she has never seen — and under it the building four miles
  down a mountain with a lamp in a cage on the fourth landing and a clerk who believed her. **She tells the man of
  fifty-one she is going west in the morning, and he says he will leave her figures up for a month and rub them out on
  the second.** **And she breaks her own sentence in the open: at the ninth hour of the fourth she tells the yard that
  she said yesterday — the third, the day before — that she was not going to walk out of this ground, and she is
  walking out of it.** 437 days.
- **ch 648, day 1,997–2,003, *What The Ninth Took Away*. SEVERANCE 9. THE BATCH'S ONE BRACKET.** The tenth hour, in
  the open, with the ground looking on and a line already written in a fair hand. Ten seconds and no sound. **THE COST,
  NAMED ONCE: she cannot go back to not knowing. Not the book and not the brother and not the name — knowing stops
  being a road back and un-knowing is nobody's work.** She tries it on the sixth day and the name comes back word for
  word, the same as a road. The woman of forty-five tells her a true thing twice and it does not arrive, and says so
  herself. The long hold still costs her four days of work. 437–443 days.
- **ch 649, day 2,004–2,010, *The Sky Says Nothing Back*. EMOTIONAL CONSEQUENCE.** Seven mornings of lying on the flat
  with her ear to the ground, and there is no tenth stage and no announcement and no eighth minute of any hour. **The
  layer announced her three times and the third was the last one it had, and she was the last address it ever had.**
  She stands on the nine paces by six one last time and does not stop on it. She cannot say the silence out loud in a
  yard, and she will not put it on a leaf. **At the fourth hour of the seventeenth she walks out of the east gate for
  the last time.** 444–450 days.
- **ch 650, day 2,011–2,018, *Whether Staying Is Keeping*. REVELATION.** The man with the shut notebook on a rise
  west of the wall. She asks him one question, standing. **No — staying is not being kept, because a kept thing has
  somebody who put it somewhere, and she has never once been anywhere on purpose that she was put in.** He has watched
  it turn into keeping three times and gives her two of them, the second being a man who stayed on a ground for
  nineteen years because a fifth of a day came off his work and nobody ever told him. He will not give her the third.
  She works out that not being kept is worse than being kept. 451–458 days.
- **ch 651, day 2,019–2,026, *The Road Stops At The Turn*. COST.** Wei Ran comes out of the west with an empty cart and
  the number is said out loud going and coming: six coppers the four miles. **That is the second and last time anybody
  has carried her for money in ten years.** **The bed at the mill is finished, so the last rate on this ground ends, and
  Wei Ran says she is not going to ask her for the last mile and would have said yes.** She pays six without counting
  and gets three, then two, then three. She walks five days west and sees her own face in a shuttered window and is
  not pleased with it. 459–466 days.
- **ch 652, day 2,027–2,033, *A Fair Hand And A Cough*. EMOTIONAL CONSEQUENCE.** **Feng Lushi is sixty-seven.** He was
  sixty-two and a hundredweight of coal came up a road in four days five years ago, and he has got four years out of
  it and has never said the number out loud. There is blood in a cloth in the side of his coat. He has not asked her
  one question since the ninety-seventh day and does not ask her one now. **She asks him whether there is a thing in
  the world that can show a person what another person sees.** He has had one in a drawer for five years — he refused it
  before the coal, in front of her, and the reason was a hundredweight of coal and a chair at the second hour and his
  chest. He says the one word for it once and spends it. **At the fourth hour he writes one line on the day's sheet and
  turns it round so she can read it, which is not necessary and is not allowed.** Six days of not asking him anything.
  467–473 days.
- **ch 653, day 2,034–2,038, *The Instrument In His Hands*. THE MIDPOINT. IRREVERSIBLE.** **MIDPOINT: HE TAKES IT AND
  THERE IS NO GIVING IT BACK.** Three things first, standing: it shows him what she is looking at, it costs the person
  who has it on about a year, and he is going to look at him and not at her. He asks her to think of him for an hour and
  three quarters in a lit room at the end of his bench, and tells her what he is asking for before she does it. She does
  it for eighty minutes without losing it once. **The cost to him is immediate: he cannot get up off a stool without a
  hand on the bench, and the drawer is shut for the rest of his life.** Four days of writing; he coughs; he finishes at
  the third hour of the fifteenth and tells her to have it alone and not that night. 474–478 days.
- **ch 654, day 2,039–2,043, *What He Wrote While He Can*. REVELATION.** He writes the last of it at the sixth hour of
  the sixteenth with the light on it and reads every sentence as he puts it down, because on the second afternoon she
  caught him saying the first one to an empty room. **THE WRITING: a man of nineteen who has been nineteen for
  nineteen years and is the fourth piece of a thing with a name the clerk will not write; burned out at a fire in the
  east; a levy of one in twenty that is not for the ground and was made to find people like her; a girl who worked all
  of it out with a stick; two hands holding a name that do not need her; and a seventh sentence — a thing seen truly and
  written truly is the only instrument in the world that can take a person through a wall, it is not the layer's, and
  nobody writes one twice.** The boy of sixteen copies two pages four feet away for an hour and a half and does not look
  up. 479–483 days.
- **ch 655, day 2,043–2,045, *A Light That Did Not Stop*. IRREVERSIBLE: HE IS DEAD.** The boy of sixteen carries the
  quarter's bundle down four flights and back up empty. **She is out of the tower when it happens, and that is by four
  hours and two days, and she had asked for it to be that way.** The boy finds her on a paving stone and says *he has
  stopped* and had been standing there eight minutes. **Nothing is shown of the end.** A keeper's lamp burns while the
  keeper is in the building and she puts the cloth over it and leaves it burning. She takes a handle at the foot of the
  board at the burial and puts him in the ground with her own hands, and not one word is said to her about it. The
  fifth item in his own column is walled behind four county copies and no one has ever sent for it. 483–485 days.
- **ch 656, day 2,046–2,050, *Rice Paper And One Hand*. DECISION, AND IT CANNOT BE UN-READ.** She opens the drawer at
  the fourth hour of the twenty-third, alone, in an empty copying room with a lamp burning that ought not to be. Seven
  sentences across four sheets. **The fifth one stops her, and it is about a girl, and it is the first true thing
  anybody has ever written about her in a hand that is not hers.** She cannot put one word of it into her own book,
  and she cannot say it out loud in the room — she gets four words and stops. She takes the four sheets out of the
  drawer at the sixth hour of the twenty-seventh and folds them inside her coat, says out loud that she is taking them
  and why, and does not write one word of it on any of the five blank leaves. 486–490 days.
- **ch 657, day 2,051–2,054, *The Four Things That Were True*. REVELATION.** **She reads all seven out loud at the
  sixth hour in daylight with a boy of sixteen in the room who was not asked and who did not lower his eyes.**
  Four things, in order: he was the fourth piece of a thing with a name and what woke up was the piece that was not
  burned; the thing is not gone and has said nothing in nineteen years; the levy is not for the ground; the name is in
  two hands that do not need her. **And a fifth thing with no word on it at all: forty-one strokes down the margin of
  the fourth sheet that Feng saw and would not write one word about, and that she is not going to find out.** She tells
  the boy to leave the fifth item where it is and not to index it. 491–494 days.
- **ch 658, day 2,055–2,056, *The Thing She Does Not Un-Say*. IRREVERSIBLE: SHE STAYS.** She goes back up the four
  flights at the sixth hour of the second. **She tells the boy of sixteen she is going to say one thing and he cannot
  leave, and he stays.** She says the thing she has never said to him: **for eight years she did not have a man, she
  had a column, and she has never once asked him a question that was not about a number, and she thought that was
  respect.** Then she says the staying, once, in daylight, standing, in a lit room with a witness in it. **He tells her
  what it costs and then tells her the new part: a thing said to two people is a thing that has happened, and she
  cannot un-stay it by walking and cannot un-stay it by un-knowing.** 495–496 days.
- **ch 659, day 2,057–2,058, *Walking Back Into The Rows*. EMOTIONAL CONSEQUENCE, AND IT SETTLES NOTHING.** She walks
  east out of Lowwater and past the rows on the second day and does not stop on the crest, as she did nine years ago,
  and goes in. **A man of nine hundred years old with a shut book goes past her on the flat at nine feet and neither of
  them says one word, and he says the fifth sentence he has ever said to her and it is about a man of nine hundred and
  it is not about himself.** She says his name out loud in the open, which is the second time and the first in front of
  people. She says the levy out loud in a ground of two hundred people. **And her brother tells her he found out about
  the fraction at three and has not said a word to anybody in nine years because if one of them knew, there would be
  nothing on the rows in a month.** She asks him no question. She does not say the other thing. **He does not say her
  name.** **She walks west off the crest in the dark with eleven miles of made road in front of her** — the map's
  figure for the gate-to-turn leg, and the figure the review repair put there in place of a wrong thirteen. 497–498
  days.
- **ch 660, day 2,059–2,060, *A Board With Nothing Written On It*. THE STAYING. THE END OF VOLUME 11.** **Seventeen**
  miles in the dark from the barrow ground to the turn, arriving at the first hour with the board on two posts bare —
  four off the rows, two of wet ground with a rut in it, the rest made road from the gate. The woman
  of sixty says out loud that she is not going to give her a third copper and is not going to ask her for one. **She
  gets the whole of it out in her own head at the third hour and it is two coppers she cannot count and five blank
  leaves and four sheets of a fair hand.** She looks up and there is nothing over her that anybody can point at and
  nothing in the sky that has ever been announced. She stays on the turn through the whole of the sixth and through the
  night because there is no shed at the turn. **At the sixth hour of the seventh she says, once, out loud, standing, in
  daylight: *I am going up*** — and he says *say the rest of it*, and she says she has not got the rest of it, and he
  says that is the first thing she has said in five hundred days that is not a figure. She says she is not going up
  because he is up there and she does not know whether he is. **In this speech the last of the ceiling went into the
  ground *sixty-three days ago* in front of *thirteen* people** — the review repair corrected a *two years* that was
  out by a factor of eighteen and a headcount of forty that ten other chapters contradict. **She did not say what was
  going to answer. The sky did not. He was still talking.** 499–500 days.

### What Volume 11 closes, and what Volume 12 inherits

**Severance 9, held at day 1,997, the fourth of the month beginning 1,994, eighty days after Severance 8 and a hundred
and fifty-five days after Severance 7. Its cost, named once at ch 648: she cannot go back to not knowing.** The ninth is
the last stage in this realm and the layer has said nothing since and never will. She has read the whole of what he was
in a dead clerk's fair hand, out loud, in daylight, in front of a boy of sixteen, and she has said out loud that she is
staying, and neither can be taken back. **A founder's fair hand is in her coat against her ribs and five blank leaves
are still five blank leaves.** Her brother is walked back to, twice now, and has not forgiven her and has not. Wei Ran's
rate has ended and the road behind it ended with it. The Hunter's request is pending and he does not raise it. Shuyin
went past her on a flat and was not thanked. **The sky is the only thing in the world nobody has read.**

## VOLUME 11 — *What He Was*, ch 601–660 — THE VOLUME IN ONE COMPACT PASS (Volume 11 close)

**Sixty chapters, 118,748 words of body prose on method B (per file: every line not beginning `#`, counted per file
and summed), average 1,979 a chapter, day 1,831 to day 2,060. Batch 0001 29,284 · Batch 0002 29,748 · Batch 0003
29,966 · Batch 0004 29,750. Three bracketed layer lines in sixty, at ch 616, ch 632 and ch 648. Severance 6 → 9 at
day 1,842 · 1,917 · 1,997, intervals 70 · 75 · 80. The seventh frame scene at ch 644. The four batch blocks above are
the per-chapter record; this is the volume in one pass, with the close's corrected figures and its repairs folded in.
Full audit in `state/continuity.md` under ***VOLUME 11 CLOSE — What He Was, ch 601–660***.**

**BATCH 0001 — ch 601–615, day 1,831–1,841, ASKING HIM WHAT HE WAS.** Nine coppers she cannot count · a bar on a
shoulder with an arm open four days in five · eleven days at a turn with nothing on the board · bread earned by trade
at a store woman's rate · a brother walked west past her and let go · **ch 606 she decides the question is askable ·
ch 609 she asks it out loud, alone, in the dark, and it cannot be un-asked, and he names the terms instead: one at a
time, in daylight · ch 615 she says them on the stones at the ninth hour and does not write them down.** Ends with a
furnace, a number and nineteen, and a place to walk to.

**BATCH 0002 — ch 616–630, day 1,842–1,916, BEING TOLD.** **ch 616 SEVERANCE 7 and its cost, named once: she cannot
hear what she is told about him ·** a name that will not stay in her head said on a made road · a shut book opened
once in nineteen years and a clerk's good hand · **ch 624 she writes `YUAN SHENG` in her own book and it cannot be
put back un-written ·** a trade and not a kindness · **ch 630 the harvest named truly at the midpoint, and the telling
misses, and the bill is hers.** Ends with her wanting the morning.

**BATCH 0003 — ch 631–645, day 1,917–1,996, KNOWING.** **ch 632 SEVERANCE 8 and its cost, named once: she cannot keep
him by asking ·** six days of one question · a shown place walked to its end · **ch 636 and ch 640 two more mouths, a
clerk's fair hand and a kiln boy grown old ·** one in twenty and a lever rubbed out again · **ch 644 the seventh frame
scene and the answer that a fragment is not used up by being known · ch 645 she tells him the tax is designed, in front
of thirteen people, and stays.** Ends with him going to stop being an entry.

**BATCH 0004 — ch 646–660, day 1,997–2,060, STAYING.** **ch 648 SEVERANCE 9 and its cost, named once: she cannot go
back to not knowing, and the layer says nothing after it ·** the sky announced to nobody, named once at ch 649 and not
explained · staying is not being kept · the last rate on that ground ends and the road behind it · **ch 652–654 Feng
Lushi sees him and takes the Lung and writes seven sentences in a fair hand — the fifth and last origin mouth and the
only one written down · ch 655 he dies ordinary · ch 656 she reads it alone and cannot be un-read · ch 657 she learns
the whole of it in daylight and does not leave · ch 658 she stays, out loud, once · ch 659 the brother, walked back to
a second time, and a man of nine hundred past at nine feet · ch 660 the turn at dawn, a bare board, seventeen miles in
the dark, and *I am going up*.** Ends with him still talking.

### The six irreversible steps, all verified in fact

**ch 609** the asking · **ch 624** `YUAN SHENG` written, four capital strokes, no solvent and no hand · **ch 645** the
tax named as designed and she stays · **ch 653** the instrument taken and spent in the moment it is in his hands ·
**ch 655** he dies, ordinary, and it cannot be un-died · **ch 658** she stays knowing and it cannot be un-stayed.

### The fourteen repairs the Volume 11 close made, in one line each

**ch 648:89** the volume's and the manuscript's only named weekday, *a Tuesday*, → *an ordinary day* · **ch 654:113**
a going-lighter out by three, *four hundred and eighty* → *four hundred and eighty-three* · **ch 660:5** the gate-to-wall
leg returned to *the two miles of flat*, which ch 660:7, ch 618 and the Volume 10 spine all say it is · **ch 649:117
and :121** the two legs transposed so the volume has **one wall** and not two · **ch 643:9** *thirteen miles of made
road* → *eleven*, the map's figure, the same rule the Batch 0004 repair applied at ch 659:119 · **ch 624:111, ch
637:7, ch 621:93, ch 621:25, ch 627:57, ch 624:9, ch 633:97, ch 642:51** eight duplicate section headings renamed, so
Volume 11 now carries zero.

### The seven findings the close review raised, and what became of them

**Six were corrections to the record and one was not upheld. No chapter file was edited by this repair and no sentence
was softened.** Full record in `state/continuity.md` under ***VOLUME 11 CLOSE — REVIEW REPAIR***.

**CORRECTED — `forgiv` is 1 across ch 601–660, not 0.** ch 659's *there is not one thing she could say about it that he
would forgive*, a denial, and the only instance in sixty chapters — the inflected `forgive`, which is why a
`\bforgiv\b` count read 0 while the substring `grep -oi forgiv` named at the close record reads 1. By batch **0 · 0 · 0
· 1**. **It forgives nothing** and the permissions at ch 609 and ch 645 are still permissions. Corrected at
`state/current.md`, `state/continuity.md` ×4, `state/open-threads.md` ×2 and the Volume 12 prompt ×2.

**CORRECTED — `the year` is 2, not 0.** ch 618 names the year of non-forgiveness inside an explicit denial of it, and
ch 637 uses an unrelated sense. By batch **0 · 1 · 1 · 0**. **Both refuse it; neither retires it.**

**CORRECTED — longest Volume 11 title is 36 characters, not 39.** *What She Said To A Woman With A Book*, ch 621. **39
is the manuscript-wide longest and belongs to Volume 8's ch 426**; the `# ` prefix had been counted in and then
mislabelled. Volume 11's titles run 2 to 9 words, not the 3 to 7 previously claimed.

**CORRECTED — Batch 0003's average was split 1,997 / 1,998.** 29,966 ÷ 15 = 1,997.73, so both were defensible and the
live line had been changed without the other four. **Four sites corrected to 1,997** and the convention named:
**averages are truncated, not rounded.** The volume's own average already followed it — 118,748 ÷ 60 = **1,979**.

**CORRECTED — stale imperatives removed from the live bullets.** `state/current.md` still told an incoming phase to do
work the close had already done. Both discharged instructions deleted and replaced with resolved records.

**CORRECTED — `state/INDEX.md` was a volume stale and the close had not touched it.** It announced the volume *closed
and audited at ch 600* while the phase being dispatched is Volume 12 planning. It now names Volume 12 planning, gives
the read order, and lists four things that may not be re-opened.

**NOT UPHELD — ch 659's eleven miles of made road, and nothing was repaired.** ch 659:119's *eleven miles of made road
in front of her* was set against ch 659:11's *four miles short of the wall*. **They are not the same moment.** Line 11
is the second hour of the fifth, walking east. Line 119 is after she has stood twenty minutes on the crest above the
rows and turned west, **and the rows are four miles past the wall**, so the entire gate-to-turn leg is ahead of her. The
sentence is true. **The residual looseness in ch 659:11 and :19 — calling her position *the long flat*, when four miles
short of the wall is two miles short of the gate and therefore made road — is real, is recorded, and is left, because
repairing it means softening a sentence.**
